# OWA Robot Test — Coverage Matrix, dated history

The dated `matrixVersion` entries that headed [coverage-matrix.md](./coverage-matrix.md) from 2026-11-15a back to the first, moved here unchanged on 2026-11-14 (`AD-33`; 2026-11-13l and before) and on 2026-11-19 (2026-11-15a to 2026-11-13k), newest first. History: each is true about its day. New entries go on top of `coverage-matrix.md`; when its head passes about 20, the oldest move to the top of this file.

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-11-15a** (**PL-13, PM-100, ST-13 -- the path editor's show/hide is a button, in every language.** Robot run 20261115-1542 found it mouse-only: a clickable `div` with no role, no focus and no key handler, so a keyboard user could not open the editor of a folder already set (a missing one is forced open). Its chevron is a real `<button>` now (Tab, Enter or Space, `aria-expanded`), its click bubbling to the row's, and the row is NOT the button -- it holds the ⋮ button, and a button's children are hidden from a screen reader. Its title, **[en:tran:Show path editor]** / **[en:tran:Hide path editor]**, had been English in every language (tips `TP-01`); both are dictionary keys now, in all 87 packages. No row added, 1155 in all.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-11-15** (**NAV-12 — the Bible Reader's 🖥️ [en:tran:Go Back to Presenter] asks first.** The user: _elderly user might click the button by accident and quit the reader. give a confirm asking be leaving reader page. give enough information about leaving and how to come back from presenter page_. **[en:tran:Leave the Bible Reader?]** says the passages stay and draws the Presenter's 📖 [en:tran:Bible Reader] tab as the way back; **[en:tran:Stay in Bible Reader]**, Esc and ✕ stay; Enter does nothing. Seen live 2026-11-15 on the dev app in English: the question, its words, the tab and both buttons; what each button does is held by the unit tests. No row added.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-11-14u** (**SP-23 -- the macOS desktop the card draws is the real one.** The user: _mini-screen previewer, keep showing the real desktop wallpaper even there is background, so I can test background color transparency_, then _let say I want it always show desktop real image_. On macOS 27 `System Events` answered `missing value` for the default aerial, so every card kept the checkered pattern, a background over it or not; the read now asks `NSWorkspace` per display id (no Apple Event, no prompt), a `.madesktop` gives its still, and a HEIC -- every system wallpaper -- is decoded by QuickLook. Seen live 2026-11-14 on the dev app: the default wallpaper behind a gradient fill whose second colour is see-through. No row added, 1155 in all.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-11-07d** (**PM-117 amended — Slide Margin split into ↔ and ↕, linked by default, and a Position pad for the lyric box.** The user: _split to vertical and horizontal, linked by default_, _add alignment support (like foreground properties)_, _align the whole box (not text alignment)_, _should rename to `Slide Margin`_, _position center by default and filled by default_, _the box size have to be the same with center one_, _change % 20 to 99 the `Slide Margin`_. Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-10-24c** (**GL-22, NAV-17, NAV-18, RD-123 and ST-53 amended.** The rows the path-settings run (20261024-1009) had not driven yet, driven on a fifth scratch profile, found three more faults, each seen live, fixed and seen again: (1) a document dropped on a list with NO folder (its path row **[en:tran:Unset Directory Path]**) was copied by its bare name into the app's WORKING directory -- the repository in dev, wherever the app was started from in a packaged build -- with no word said: the drop handler waited for a path of `null`, and a list with no folder answers `''`; it now says **Please open a folder first**, and `fsCopyFilePathToPath` refuses an empty or relative folder for every caller; (2) **File → Export Data** failed whenever a document had unsaved edits -- its live head was written into a staging folder with no `documents/` in it (ENOENT) -- and left the half-built tar, no manifest, in Downloads under the archive's own name; the folder is made first, and a failed export removes what it wrote; (3) on a first run with no Bible the Reader asked **[en:tran:Create the KJV Bible?]** again from a card mounted while the first question was up; **[en:tran:Create the provided KJV]** made the KJV and left the second up, asking for a Bible that was there -- and a window shows one confirm at a time, so **[en:tran:Copy to Data Directory]** then seemed to do nothing, its question queued behind; one question per window now; (4) **Import Data**'s result, _Imported N file(s); M already up to date_, was the one line of the import not through `tran()` -- English in a Khmer or French window -- and is translated now. The four fixes were then driven again in KHMER on a profile whose data folder is on a `subst` stick: one KJV question, the no-folder drop said in Khmer, an export with an unsaved document, and the import back onto the stick reading _បាននាំចូលឯកសារ 3; 1 មានរួចហើយ_, with no missing-translation error in either console. Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-10-24b** (**ST-22, ST-82 and MD-01 amended.** Found on the follow-up pass of the same path-settings run (20261024-1009), one scratch profile driven through the whole Desktop cycle and a data folder on a `subst` stick: (1) **[en:tran:Hide for 30 days]** pressed in the start-up QUESTION left the Desktop card up in that window, saying what had just been put away -- the card read the hide once, at mount; it now hears `hideDataDirMoveForAWhile` / `showDataDirMoveAgain` in its own window and reads the hide again when a window comes to the front, and its ✕ stays that window's; (2) a video download from a URL left **"Downloading video …, please wait"** up beside **"Video downloaded successfully"** -- two toasts, the first held by the pointer resting on it, and here it covered the Ok of a **Select Default Folder** question so the press missed; video and image downloads now update ONE toast, as the audio one did; (3) **Download From URL** on Background › Images staged its file in the DEFAULT data folder (`~/.owa/open-worship-data`): it failed with that folder absent (a data folder on a stick, a new computer) and otherwise wrote into a folder not in use -- it now stages in the OS temp folder like the videos and audios, and never replaces an image already there; (4) the **Put away for 30 days** list under **Other General Options** was read once, when General opened, so the Settings card's own **[en:tran:Hide for 30 days]** -- or a tip put away in another window -- left no **[en:tran:Show again]** to press until Settings was reopened; the list now reads again when told and when the window comes to the front. Seen live: the card gone at once after the question's Hide; an image downloaded onto the stick with the default folder's listing and time unchanged and one toast on screen; the row listed at once after the Settings card's Hide; the card's ✕ closing that window's card only, a later focus not bringing it back and nothing written. No row-count change.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-10-24a** (**ST-15, ST-53, ST-80, ST-83 amended.** Found by a robot run over the data-folder settings on a scratch dev profile (`/owa-robot-test` path settings, run 20261024-1009), the user's goal: _make sure all path and data dir functionalities work perfectly_. Four faults, each seen live, fixed and seen again: (1) a path typed into the **[en:tran:Parent Directory:]** box that does not exist was MADE and used -- one typing slip created an empty data folder with `local-storage` and `bibles-data` and switched to it; now it asks **[en:tran:Create Data Folder]** (Cancel puts the box back, nothing made), and a path that is not a whole one (`C:`, `data`) is refused with a toast naming an example; (2) the box's blur after Enter committed the same path again, so every question came twice, and a new folder answered No to **[en:tran:Set according paths]** was asked again by the page that rendered next -- now once per folder per window; (3) emptying the box and leaving it stopped using the data folder with no question, every window showing **[en:tran:No Parent Directory Selected]** -- now the box goes back to the folder in use; (4) **[en:tran:Copy Data Folder]** kept raw paths that named the ORIGINAL folder, so on the copy the Documents setting and a slide's picture read and wrote the original (Repair Links could not see it, the original being there) -- the copy now rewrites them to `$DATA_DIR_PATH` as the move does. Also: the move question named `~/.owa/open-worship-data` even when that folder held another data folder; it names the folder the move will make (`… (1)`), and asking makes nothing. The empty first-run box no longer carries a red mark. Seen live: typed missing folder → Cancel (nothing made) → Create (made, asked once); `C:` refused; emptied box unchanged; a copy of a folder with raw links switched to and reading its own documents; the question naming `open-worship-data (3)`; all in Khmer with no missing key.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-10-23f** (**GL-25 amended — Help → Tips of the Day and All tips open in every open page with tips.** Asked for by the user: _open `Help` menubar, when click show in all pages_, then _the tip of the day that opened by `Help` menu should have no `Hide for 30 days`_. The click is sent to every window (`isSentToAllWindows` → `sendMenuClickedToAllWindows`) and `DailyTipComp` no longer waits for its window to be in front. Before, a popup with its menu bar hidden could never be asked for its own tips. A card asked for from Help hides the link (`isAskedFor`); the automatic card keeps it. Seen live 2026-10-23 with the Reader and Settings open: both cards up from one press of each item, neither with the link, the chatbot window untouched.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-10-23e** (**ST-20, ST-54, ST-55 amended; ST-82..ST-86 and CB-163 new — a data folder is required, and only ever chosen by a press.** Asked for by the user over one long session, with Path Settings empty and the Reader still listing an old folder's Bibles and notes: _while no data dir selected there should no data to display at all_, _parent data dir selected is a requirement_, _the default folder path should be just a suggestion, only user click to choose (create if not exist)_, _should recommend only dir found in ~/.owa and drives_, _the data moving warning card should be in all pages_, _tell user it is not a safe place_, _in path setting, add option to copy data dir to a drive or to a folder_, then the goal: _make data dir selection and data moving to safe place work for user, chatbot can help user understand about this, no regression_. With no data folder in use -- none chosen, or the chosen one not there -- every window but Settings shows only that one must be chosen, with Go to Settings and App Assistant; Settings opens on Path Settings (now its default tab) with only General (language, theme) and AI Providers beside it and no automatic tip. Path Settings suggests ~/.owa/open-worship-data (Use This Folder makes it) and lists the data folders at the top of the drives, never one on the Desktop; a missing chosen folder is only marked Missing in Recent Folders; Copy Data Folder copies the folder in use, verified, to a place the person picks. A Desktop data folder in use puts a card in every window saying the Desktop is not a safe place, with Move Now and Hide for 30 days. The two start-up dialogs (Data Folder Not Found, Data Folder Found) are gone. The chatbot opened from a window works in that window, Settings has an App Assistant button, and the corpus carries the why and the how of the data folder. Fixed on the way: unsetting the folder wrote the cleared child paths into a RELATIVE `local-storage/` in the process's working folder, the default folder's button asked about its standard folders twice at once, and a popup's Tip of the Day read its opener's launch time and came up at once -- the wait is now per page. And the settings the user listed as the data folder's (SongSelect, stay-awake, Screen Mirror and virtual displays, the note editor) are kept in it, routed by the main process through the same calls -- the AI assistants, the language, secrets and the mirror's identity, router mapping and last port stay the computer's; seen end to end on a scratch profile with the Desktop move and Copy Data Folder. Rows: 86 ST, 163 CB.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-10-23a** (**CB-161 new — a passage's chat finds everything related to it.** Asked for by the user with a Bible view's 🤖 conversation about Genesis 8:1-22 circled: _make the chatbot to be super smart that can answer everything about selected verses_, and the goal _help user find everything related to the selected verses_. A passage's card gathers the app's own cross references (the Reader's Cross References data, in the Bible's language) and its chapter's Resources files as it opens and says how many; **Related verses** and **Study files for this chapter** list them with no model; the card's first question asks for the related verses; a question about related verses is answered from them, quoted from the reader's Bible; a passage read in that chat defaults to the card's Bible. The other Testament is also read off the chapters around a passage (Jesus on the days of Noah, under Genesis 8); an answer that says the Bible does not say it, with nothing read, goes back once; and an abort nobody pressed Stop for is said as a failure, never in silence. A question naming a verse by number has that verse's own cross references read first, and one about before, after or a length of time has the verses around the passage read first. Measured on `passage-khmer-asks.json`, 15 questions, gemma4:cloud in Khmer: 4 / 4 / 7 → 15 / 0 / 0 right / partly / wrong in two runs, English shown 3 → 0. One row added.)

A book named in the words, even misspelt, is the book searched; a search says where its words gather (the chapters a story is told in) and how many each book holds; **Every verse with these words** pages the whole search; a chapter an answer names whole opens whole; a story asked for by its name reaches the verse finder in English and Khmer; a verse quoted that no search returned goes back to the model once; and Find's 🤖 with words typed searches them at once. Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-10-22d** (**DD-01..DD-19 re-driven with the new tools on a scratch dev instance, and three more defects fixed.** Every row was run again through fresh stdio servers on a second dev instance with its own data folder, by `owa_drag` (sealed) and `owa_drop_files`, each read back with a data tool, the disk or a screenshot. Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-10-19j** (**RD-29 and RD-122 — every Bible preview section has a color, lime by default.** Asked for by the user, with the pane's color dot circled: _all bible preview section should have color_ … _when not setting found for the color of the editing section then it should be `lime`_. `BibleItemsViewController.getColorNote` returns the default lime for an item with no stored note (one canonical lowercase spelling, so a picked lime and the default are one group), so an untouched pane shows `Color Note: lime`, joins the lime group (sync-scroll toggle, one passage), and its split buttons ask a version; `No Color` resets a chosen color to lime. Seen live 2026-10-19 on the dev Reader in Khmer: two default-lime panes grouped, a split asking a version and joining, both panes following one reference, red → `No Color` back to lime without a reload. The split-at-once branch is unit tested only — no preview pane reports no color now.)

Seen live 2026-10-19 on the dev chatbot in Khmer (`ពគប` installed): **GKHB (ណែនាំ)** / **គខប** / **គកស១៦** / **អគត**, `ពគប` absent, the accessibility descriptions reading the four Khmer names; the French and Thai lists are unit tested.)

Slides keep theirs in `<file>.transition.json` beside the document; backgrounds in `background-tab-transition` / `background-item-transition`; foreground in `foreground-component-transition-<widget>` and the session's own `…-setting-show-widget-transition`. Per session, the last 20, kept in one setting and read once per mount. Verified live 2026-10-04 on the dev window. Row count +1.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-10-04a** (**new PM-152: the countdown and stopwatch go up stopped and are started, paused, resumed and reset from their panels; PM-151 follows.** Asked for by the user: _"I want to be able to control like pause reset resume start... so when show it should not start yet, but should have option for auto-start"_, then _"then apply to stopwatch as well"_. The state lives in the screen datum (`pausedMillisecond`, and `durationMillisecond` for Reset), so the screen window and every mini preview read one pause; a timing-only change is handed to the clock already up (`ScreenForegroundManager.updateInPlace`) instead of remounting it. The countdown now rounds UP while counting down, so it needs no lead second and `00:00:00` arrives exactly when the time is up. Verified live 2026-10-04 on the dev window. Row count +1.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-10-03b** (**new PM-151: the countdown takes seconds, and counts the time over once it is up.** Asked for by the user with a picture of the Countdown panel: _"I want second option, and when time out I want it start counting up so I can know how long it over"_. The duration form has an **s** box after **h** and **m**; at zero the countdown flashes red five times and then counts UP behind a `+` until hidden — both forms, every way one is started. The clock ticks once a second on the second now, not on every animation frame, since a countdown no longer stops at zero. Verified live 2026-10-03 on the dev window: 0 h 0 m 7 s read `00:00:07` and then `+00:00:03` in red on the Mini Screen. Row count +1.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-10-03a** (**new CB-79: a song by its NAME.** Asked for by the user with a picture of _Create a lyric file from "Amazing Grace"_ typed into the help window: a title is looked up — the user's own songs, then the app's public-domain hymn collection, through `owa_lyric_validate` `mode: "find"` — never written from memory, offered under the same **Create** / **Copy song text** buttons; `/lyric <title>` and the offline guide do it with no assistant. The Presenter's fourth starter chip is now _Create a lyric file for song "Amazing Grace"_; the link chip is in **More…**. Verified live 2026-10-03 on the dev window. Row count +1.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-10-02d** (**new SP-24: a mini-screen card can stop drawing its screen.** Asked for by the user for low-spec machines: the eye after **FG** in each card's header (**[en:tran:Stop rendering preview]**) swaps the card's body for a static striped **[en:tran:No rendering]** face while `screen.html` keeps drawing and every control keeps working. Every drawing layer unmounts and hands its root back to its manager, which stops what it held (clips, cameras, countdown frames); the slide layer stays mounted unpainted because a slide's media sound and `Slide: Media Control` live there. Verified live 2026-10-02 on the dev presenter. SP 23 → 24, total 892 → **893**.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-10-02c** (**The run-sheet findings of robot run 20261002-1644, fixed — asked for by the user ("fix all").** PL-53: two entries of one document fold and unfold TOGETHER on screen as well as in the setting (the second one stayed drawn open). PL-50/51: a damaged entry is announced and logged once a minute, with the entry as text, not on every re-read and once per surface. PL-75: a failed Import From URL says what went wrong in one toast (`The file was not found at that address. (HTTP 404)`, `Could not reach that address…`). PL-46/PL-99: a colour line no longer stops the run on a Yes/No about the Bible text colour — it says so in a toast with **Undo** — and every popup gives the keyboard back to what held it. PL-92: the CC row's gear and stopwatch are real buttons. Row drift corrected from the same run: PL-37 (Reveal Original on an audio row OPENS the Audios split), PL-41 (the marker is the on-air `●`), PL-44 (the tree is virtualised), PL-47 (the collapse pair is bottom-LEFT), PL-59 (the preview's slide-card menu is the tree's own), PL-71/PL-74 (ELEVEN per-widget clears), PL-88 (entries carry a uuid), PL-89 (an outside payload becomes the line its CC points at). Verified live 2026-10-02 on the dev presenter. No row-count change.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-10-02b** (**PM-147, PL-69: status only for what is actually on the screen.** PL-69: a run-sheet countdown, stopwatch or quick-text row used to mark while ANY one of its kind was up, the panel's own included; it now marks only while its own replay is up, and a message row is no longer missed when a second message of the same length is up. A saved Bible passage row and the presenter's Bibles tab are matched on the passage AND its version, so a KJV row no longer lights while NIV's John 3:16 is on the screen. **PM-147: a session's Hide row is that session's own.** Reported with a picture: Session 2's **[en:tran:Marquee Bottom]** panel offered **[en:tran:Hide Marquee Bottom]** for screen 0 while the scroll there was Default's. The Hide row listed every overlay of that kind whoever put it up; it now lists the session in front's only, on the five single-slot panels and on Messages, and an overlay whose session is gone belongs to the first session button. Verified live 2026-10-02 on the dev presenter: Default's panel showed **Hide Marquee Bottom 0**, Session 2's none. No row-count change.)

CB-20 needs a new stuck step: W-06 step 3 now names a button. Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-10-01** (**Second revision of the day: the fixes from the reader-only robot run 20261001-1149.** Asked for by the user ("fix all findings", and for the High: _maybe add pencil to be one of the icons_; then _unable to scroll when overflow_). Rows rewritten to what the app does now: RD-19, RD-26 and RD-33 (the pane header: its hover actions sit inside the reference box, scroll sideways in a narrow pane, and lead with the pencil; the title's book, chapter and verses are keyboard buttons), RD-102 (graph undo from anywhere in its panel; a pressed control that goes away hands the focus to the canvas), RD-97 (**No connection found within 30 steps**), RD-57 (a name's Gender and a place's type are translated), RD-69 ("Lot the son of Haran" is left plain), RD-70 (ligature spellings match), RD-75 (each section is headed by its own pane's bible), RD-11 (`Aramaic <arc>`), RD-23 (the Word export is in bible order), RD-29 (the colour note is named), RD-32 (the title drag carries its kind), RD-44 (the shortest suggestion first), RD-47 (**1 verse found**), RD-48 (the verse as the bible prints it; the row's own name), RD-77 (the info card is a dialog), RD-85 (the Introduction tag gives way to the name), RD-108 and RD-110 (the verse-mark buttons are named), RD-119 (a read-only note's toolbar is locked), CM-65 (a duplicated note is dated now) and CM-70 (Edit Title takes the focus; drift: an inline field, not a popup). Drift fixed: RD-17 (Previous at Genesis 1 wraps to Revelation 22 by design) and RD-93 (Jacob 47 · Children 16). Verified live 2026-10-01 on the dev Reader: the strip scrolls both ways under the wheel and a scrolled button stops at the fade; Enter on `Book: Psalm` opens the book picker; the colour notes read `Color Note: magenta`; Jacob's Gender reads `ប្រុស` and Siloam's Type `ស្រះ`; Jacob → Jesus answers **No connection found within 30 steps**; Collapse then Ctrl+Z restores the box and leaves the reference box alone; Edit Title takes the focus and one Escape closes it with the note file byte-identical. RD-119's locked toolbar and the reference-box race (Enter now acts on the box's CURRENT text, and no half-typed passage reaches the history) are unit-tested, not yet re-checked live. No row-count change.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-10-01** (**Reader fixes from the reader-only robot run 20261001-0808, and seven rows that had drifted from the app.** Asked for by the user ("fix all"). Rows rewritten to what the app does: RD-21 (the keys live in Settings; the Reader keeps the auto-play megaphone), RD-39 (no Add Item button; retired), RD-48 (a plain click appends; Enter on a row works; one-pass highlight), RD-75 (heading names the pane's bible), CM-82 (Edit makes the pane the editing one), RD-86 (Preview PDF in the row menu), KB-02 (the Bible-info popup binds no Ctrl+Q on purpose). Fixed in the app and verified live 2026-10-01 on the dev Reader: Find's `lord's` highlights with no raw HTML; Next/Previous across Obadiah→Jonah gives Jonah 1:1-17 and back Obadiah 1:1-21; the new-lines switch re-wraps the open passage at once; a context-menu letter highlights its item; a new names query from page 4 lands on page 1; Edit Title then Escape leaves the note file byte-identical. No row-count change.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-30** (**CB-68 extended: several AI Chat windows, tabs that move between them and along the strip, and a Recently closed list.** Asked for by the user in five messages. Verified live 2026-09-30 on the dev window: **Open in new window** moved a blank tab and then a signed-in Claude conversation into windows of their own (the second loaded on the same conversation, still signed in, and left its first window on the card); with three windows up, **New window** opened none; a closed Claude tab showed under **Recently closed** on the card with `claude.ai · just now` and came back on its conversation; the **🕘** panel opened under the head row; a drag of **New chat** onto **Greeting - Claude** put it first. The drop outside the window is an OS drag loop CDP cannot start, so it is covered by `RenderSessionTabsComp.test.tsx` and the shared move path the menu route proved. No row-count change.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-29** (**GL-25: the Tip of the Day card takes itself off after one minute.** Asked for directly with the card circled over the Presenter's top-right controls: it no longer waits to be dismissed. A 3px bar along the card's bottom edge drains over that minute (`transform` only, so the card never repaints for it), the pointer resting on the card freezes the bar and the timer together, and leaving carries on from where the bar stopped rather than granting a fresh minute. Next tip restarts it, All tips holds it, x still closes at once. Verified live 2026-09-29 on the dev Reader and Slide Editor. No row-count change.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-28e** (**PM-149 / GL-25: the previewer's Note pane ships COLLAPSED.** Asked for directly with the two empty note boxes circled: few volunteers ever write a document or slide note, and the pane spent a seventh of the previewer on them. New **PM-149** — the green `Note` strip under the slide previews, the Document Note / Slide Note boxes behind it, the per-document memory, and the one-off close of documents that were already remembering it open. Closing a pane now also persists the grow its neighbour ABSORBED, so a close / reload / reopen round trip lands back where it started instead of shrinking the neighbour each lap. GL-25 expects 89 Presenter lessons (79 with a safe Do it), the new one being _Write a note on a document or slide_; PM-118's floating previewer carries the same collapsed pane. Verified live 2026-09-28 on the dev presenter; total 890 -> 891.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-28d** (**CM-101 expanded: font size and slide themes.** Generate Slides supports Auto or a positive pixel size plus Dark/Light palettes. Both retain the inset, translucent background and 85% upcoming size. Light-theme verse numbers keep their readable color when reopened.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-28c** (**ED-21 / ED-25: editable hex colors.** Direct entry accepts RGB/RGBA hex with optional #; Enter/blur applies, Escape cancels, invalid input restores the current value. Picker arrows and Undo/Redo verified live.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-28b** (**ED-52: Bulk Font.** Apply size or family independently to all slides or a custom selection of slides and canvas items; locked items are opt-in. One history entry per application, Undo/Redo verified live.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-28a** (**CM-101: Generate Slides from a Presenter Bible item.** Version picker, editable title and verse slides, per-version fonts, compact spacing, muted next verse at 85% size, collision-safe filenames and missing-passage handling.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-28** (**GL-25 expanded: fourteen Presenter-only lessons.** Verify lookup preparation/history/study tools, scoped media and flow library controls, Messages/rotation/spacing, Effects and Image Show preparation. All 88 Presenter tips remain searchable and page-specific; no row-count change.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-27b** (**new SP-23: each mini-screen card draws its monitor's own desktop wallpaper behind what it renders, in place of the checkered pattern.** Asked for directly. The first cut photographed the display with `desktopCapturer` and was wrong for two reasons the live run showed at once: a capture is the display AS COMPOSITED, so the card for a screen on the machine's own monitor drew a picture of the app inside itself, and it has to be retaken to stay honest -- a periodic desktop grab, plus a stale frame ghosting the previous slide behind the current one. The backdrop is now the wallpaper FILE read from the OS: no other window is in it, it is read once per display and never on a timer, and the desktop's own layout is copied. SP-18's menu list re-derived from the live menu (it had been missing `Bible Properties` too); SP 22 -> 23, total 887 -> 888.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-27a** (**new PM-148: Add Stage is the same picker the mini screen's `St:` badge opens.** Asked for directly, with both menus circled in one picture: the Stage Previewer could only ever offer Stage 1 and then go dead, while a screen's stage number increments without a ceiling — so a screen set to `St: 3` showed a render the previewer had no pane for. The two menus now share `STAGE_NUMBER_CHOICE_COUNT` and the same shape: five numbered stages with a shown one listed and disabled, then **Increment · Stage \<max+1\>** past the end of the list. The enabling change is underneath: `getLyricAppDocumentStageByStage` no longer clamps at the last layout, so stage 2 and up borrow stage 1's layout but keep their own number, their own cached instance (`getStageInstance`, the stage in the cache key) and their own `lyric-stage-style-<stage>` record — proven live by flipping stage 4's Theme to Dark and watching stage 3 stay light. Panes are capped at 8 for memory; the per-stage style parse memo is now LRU-bounded, having been documented as bounded by the two stage classes. One row added, and the Row counts section re-derived from the ids after four prefixes had drifted: 859 stated → 887 real.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-27** (**CM-100: the note item's `Discard Change`.** A Bible Notes item menu now offers `Discard Change` whenever the note FILE has an editing history with something in it — an undo/redo step, a head that differs from the file, or a head that cannot be read. Added because a truncated head made every item in that file open to a permanent spinner with NO in-app way back: the list kept working, a list write did not repair the head, and only deleting `.histories` outside the app recovered it. Verified live 2026-09-27 on a scratch note — offered on a corrupt head, absent with no history, and the note opened again after the confirm, with the saved `.own` untouched.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-26e** (**Slide Editor reconciled after the `appDocumentEditor.html` robot run and its fixes (report-20260926-1950).** GL-08 names its buttons by role (most confirms read **No / Yes**), GL-14 alerts carry an **Ok** and close on `Enter` too. ED-03 has no **Edit** in the editor, ED-12 lists **Audios**, ED-13 keeps Undo/Save under "No slide selected", ED-16/17 re-seed per slide and count from 1, ED-20 commits every press of a burst in order and has no active state, ED-21 picks an opaque colour after No Color, ED-24 shows Original Size / Strip for media only, ED-35 reads a percentage beside a named **Center view**, ED-43 refuses a locked box with a toast. New ED-49 (insert toolbar, the three link prompts translated once — they threw in Khmer), ED-50 (Document / Slide Note auto-save) and ED-51 (a YouTube box is a still in every thumbnail). Verified live 2026-09-26 on the dev editor, English and Khmer. +3 rows.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-26d** (**Settings coverage reconciled with the live app.** ST-07 now records the Clear All confirmation, ST-22 includes Daily Tips, and ST-50 records cache cleanup. The embedded SongSelect entries are restored as proper rows; its Settings card moves to unique ID ST-57 while Repair Links retains ST-52.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-26c** (**CB-77 and GL-25 expanded: seven working Reader demos and broader matching Tips.** The Reader's zero-model shelf grows from 24 to 30 safe choices: seven working actions are added for complete-reference entry, last-reference-part removal, automatic Bible audio, note filtering/sorting, Settings and Help, while the old no-op whole-panel entry is retired. All Tips grows from 54 to 61 Reader lessons, 30 with an actionable first step; the whole saved-passages panel and its saved-Bibles/Bible-Notes sections stay self-guided because an open pane is not a pressable toggle. No row-count change.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-26b** (**Four foreground defects found by a foreground-focus robot run and fixed. PM-134: a space or Enter typed AT THE END of a message was parsed straight back off** -- the editors were derived from the stored string, and that format joins messages with a blank line so it cannot carry a message's own trailing whitespace; the editors are LOCAL state now and the setting is only the persisted form. **PM-22/PR-10: a foreground camera never reached the projector** -- `renderCamera` passed the stored `deviceId` straight to `getUserMedia`, and Chromium rotates that id per origin and per SESSION, so the id the presenter saved was routinely dead in the screen window; it resolves by label first now, like the slide camera item already did, and a failure is reported to the OPERATOR'S window (never the projector, which is where a toast would land). **PM-86: the Time widget's `UTC Offset` box held HOURS while labelled `min`** -- `getSystemTimezoneHourOffset` returns `-getTimezoneOffset()/60` and `TimingController` multiplies by 3 600 000, so a volunteer typing 420 for Phnom Penh got a clock weeks out; the unit and the aria-label say hours, and `parseFloat` + `step 0.25` let India +5:30 and Nepal +5:45 work at all. **PM-89: the remove-clock control was a bare `<i onClick>`** with no name, role or tabIndex -- invisible to the accessibility tree and to `owa_find_ui`; it is a real numbered button now. PM-37's pass condition was rewritten: it still described one widget titled Foreground, which the launcher replaced. No row-count change.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-26** (**GL-25 expanded: the automatic Tip of the Day waits five minutes.** Reported with a picture of the card over the Presenter's top-right controls at launch. It now appears five minutes after launch, counted from the launch in session storage (`daily-tip-launched-at`), so a reload or page switch does not restart it; Help is never delayed, and a tip opened from Help stands in for the automatic one. No row-count change.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-25i** (**PM-146: a session's Properties act on that session's OWN overlay.** Reported straight after the strip landed. The values were already filed per session and the panel already drew the right ones; what leaked was the live restyle, which walked every overlay of that kind — so a nudge to Session 2's size or colour re-dressed the countdown Default had on the wall. Scoped to the owning session; the Hide row is deliberately NOT scoped, because there must always be a way to take something off a projector from whichever session is in front. Proven live 2026-09-25 by reading the screen's own saved style both ways round.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-25h** (**PM-146, PM-147: every foreground component keeps SESSIONS, not only the four that show a file.** Asked for directly, pointing at the Video Show panel's own `Default / +` strip and circling the components in the launcher menu. Messages, Marquee Top, Marquee Bottom, Quick Text, Countdown, Stopwatch and Time now carry the same strip, a session owning that panel's own words or numbers AND its own Properties — the pre-service notice board and the mid-service alert side by side, each already the right size in the right corner, instead of one retyped over the other minutes before a service. Default's suffix is the empty string, so it keeps writing the keys the widgets always wrote. The two widgets that hold a LIST (Messages, Time) can have two sessions up at once and preserve each other's items; the five single-slot ones hand the slot over and move the on-screen dot with it. Removing a session takes what it put up off the screens first. Verified live 2026-09-25 on the presenter.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-25h** (**PM-109: the Background Audio body was a stub, and the row only opened from part of itself.** Reported with a picture -- "restore the background audio body, it stop working now" -- and NOT caused by the folder-session rail (proven by taking the rail off: the tile stayed 134px). An audio row asks for the full width, but how many share a line is `toColumnCount(panel width, the SHARED thumbnail slider)`, so a wider split or a smaller slider put two on a line; under ~250px Chromium drops the scrubber, the clock and the volume from an `<audio controls>` and leaves a play button. Pinned to one column, and the click now finds the ROW with `closest` instead of walking up one parent -- the name line opened nothing before. Verified live 2026-09-25: full-width player, opens from anywhere on the row.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-25g** (**PM-144, PM-145: every background media tab keeps FOLDER SESSIONS, on ONE short rail.** Asked for directly, from the foreground Image Show widget that already had them -- a church keeps this Sunday's pictures on a stick and its standing set in the data folder, and reaching either meant re-pointing the ONE folder Path Settings names. Images, Videos, Webs and Audios now carry the same `Default / +` strip, each session owning its own folder, view mode, sort and slide show; the Default session keeps writing the very keys the tab always used, so nothing an existing install has set moves. The strip and the slide show share one 20px row (the foreground rail came down to the same height), which a 200px panel feels as most of a row of pictures. The strip is ABOVE the file list rather than inside its body: an empty session is exactly when the way back to another one has to be on screen. Verified live 2026-09-25 on the presenter.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-25f** (**ED-48: a canvas box casts a shadow, and there are two kinds.** Asked for directly. **Box Shadow** is the shadow of the RECTANGLE, rounded corners included -- right for a text box with a backing colour. **Drop Shadow** is `filter: drop-shadow()`, which follows what is actually PAINTED: the letters of a box with no backing, the cut-out of a logo PNG. Handing a transparent logo a box shadow draws a rectangle in mid-air behind it, which is why this is a picker and not a checkbox. It lives in `genShapeBoxStyle`, unlike the blend, so the editor canvas, the slide thumbnails, the projector and the print PDF all agree. **No Shadow** stores nothing at all. One row added.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-25e** (**PM-140: the Webs background list runs a slide show too, and its tiles mark the page that is up.** Asked for directly. This tab could not borrow the Images/Videos walk: half its tiles are saved URLs rather than files, and both kinds are grouped by colour note together, so it walks an order of its own (`genBackgroundWebDisplayedSrcList`) and the show steps URL items as readily as files. Its file tiles carried the same stale badge PM-139 fixes, for the same reason and through the same marker; its URL tiles never did, not being memoised. The strip is drawn only while a web page is on a screen, and that decision moved out of CSS: a wrapper that stays in the tree keeps its row whatever it holds, so the card now keeps the strip out of the tree entirely. One row.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-25d** (**PM-137..PM-139: one slide-show strip, its rules opened in place, and a grid that marks what is actually up.** The control is the same compact segmented strip on all three surfaces -- the slides previewer, the background lists and the foreground media panels -- and the floating one stopped being a full-width band across the bottom of the previewer, which had blacked out a row of the very slides it was stepping through. Its rules open UNDER the bar rather than over the panel: Repeat All, a jumping Step, a Random Up To ceiling whose draw is written into the seconds box before each wait, and, for clips, Wait Until The Video Ends, which shows the clip's own length in whole seconds. The background strip is drawn only while that list has something on a screen, since a show cannot start from nothing. And the tile marking was wrong wherever it mattered: a memoised tile reads "am I on a screen?" from the managers, which no prop carried, so the item now showing stayed unmarked and the one before it kept its highlight until something remounted the list. Three rows.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-25c** (**PM-134..PM-136: Messages is a session of message editors, and Mask.** Alert and Announcements shipped as two widgets, were merged on sight (side by side they were the same panel showing the same thing in the same place), and the merged one then turned out to be a single text box where a message could not span lines. It is now ONE session holding SEVERAL editors: each editor is a multi-line message with its own Show / move / remove, several can be on a screen at once, and **[en:tran:Show All Messages]** puts the whole session up — as one block, or one message at a time with **[en:tran:Rotate]**. Two things only the live screen showed: two messages rendered in the SAME box until they were laid out by the LINES above them, and a multi-line message rendered only its first line until the renderer learned that `textList` is entries when rotating and lines when not. Every per-message control carries its number, because three unnumbered ones give a screen reader and `owa_click` one name three times. The mask is unchanged: static percentage bars pinned above every layer, which `Clear All` deliberately does not remove. Three rows.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-24b** (**ED-47: every canvas item kind carries a Blend Mode.** The picker sits under Shape Properties beside Glass Effect and Round, and shares the foreground widgets' vocabulary. The blend group is the SLIDE, not the screen: a lone blended box over the screen background shows no change, which is correct and has to be said. One row added.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-24a** (**PM-132..PM-133: the Foreground is a menu of components, each in its own floating panel.** The chooser opens at the cursor and reports which panels are open, what is on a screen and where, and which slide show is running; a media panel's sessions / Properties / slide show / screen buttons are one pinned row over a grid that fills the panel. PM-128 and PM-129 re-worded off the old collapse-a-card model. Two rows added.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-24** (**GL-25 expanded: every native menu is now learnable.** Presenter grows from 56 to 61 lessons and Reader from 49 to 54. Five searchable self-guided overviews cover every command under File, Edit, Tools, Window, and Help; the seven detailed View lessons remain. No row-count change.)

The LT section said a static source grep could not find a missing Khmer key. `src/lang/tranKeyCoverage.test.ts` now does, in `npm run lint` (`EN-20`: five keys on failure paths had none), so LT-02 remains the only catch for DYNAMIC keys. Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-17g** (**RD-121 amended: a Mermaid diagram OPENS in the Mermaid Live Editor.** Asked for by the user with the copy menu's three Mermaid rows circled in a picture: _as a user I want to be able to open each copy mermaid diagram string in mermaid live editor directly, check how mermaid live share link work._ Copying a diagram is half of what a diagram is for; nothing on an ordinary machine DRAWS one but this app's own markdown preview. **Open in Mermaid Live** now sits under a divider at the foot of that menu and opens a second menu with the three Mermaid shapes. The link is the editor's own `serializeState` written out — JSON state, UTF-8, zlib deflate, URL-safe unpadded base64, `pako:` in the FRAGMENT, which never reaches a server, so nothing is uploaded — with its documented `base64:` tag as the fallback where `CompressionStream` is missing, and a measured 8 000 character ceiling past which the link is copied with a sentence instead, because `shell.openExternal` drops a longer one in silence while still resolving. 7 unit tests, the `pako` payload inflated back through Node's zlib so the encoding is proven against a decoder this app does not own. Verified live 2026-09-17 on David's graph: the row drew unclipped, Chrome opened the editor, and the same link read back through `owa_read_website` showed the editor holding this app's diagram with its Khmer names intact. A pre-existing break in this row was repaired in the same pass: a `\n` written into it had landed as a real newline and split the table row in two. No row-count change.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-17f** (**RD-121 amended: FIVE diagram languages on the copy menu, not one.** Asked for by the user: _add variety of diagrams, so this will let user to have more options to see many format of diagrams they want_. `GRAPH_DIAGRAM_FORMAT_LIST` declares them once — Mermaid flowchart across, Mermaid flowchart down, Mermaid mindmap, Graphviz DOT, PlantUML — and the menu, the toast and `buildGraphDiagram` all read that list, so a sixth is a row plus a branch. The mindmap needed a spanning TREE (`toChildKeyListByKey`, breadth-first and undirected from the centre, unreachable records parked on the centre) and deliberately carries no relation words. Live run also found a UI defect the first cut shipped with: a context menu in this app is 210px and CLIPS, so `Copy as Mermaid Flowchart (left to right)` and `… (top down)` both drew as `Copy as Mermaid Flowchar…` — two identical rows doing different things. Rows are named short now, with the full name on the hover (`ContextMenuItemType.title`) and on the toast. Verified live 2026-09-17 on Jacob's graph (13 children, Judah expanded): six distinct rows, and all three Mermaid shapes rendered in the app's own markdown preview. DOT and PlantUML were checked against their grammars and by unit tests only — neither `dot` nor a PlantUML renderer exists on this machine, and the app draws Mermaid only. 22 tests on the builders. No row-count change.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-17e** (**RD-121 amended: the Markdown copy carries the diagram in a `mermaid` fence.** Asked for by the user right after the first cut: _the markdown should include mermaid `mermaid ... ` for the diagram_. `buildGraphMarkdown` now calls `buildGraphMermaid` and opens the document with a `## Diagram` section holding it fenced, ahead of the tables — a document about a graph should show the graph, and everywhere markdown is actually rendered that block draws the same boxes the canvas has. Two guards: the fence grows to four backticks or more past any run inside the diagram (a record label carrying ``` would otherwise end the block and spill the rest into the page as prose), and a graph with no records writes no diagram at all rather than fencing a `flowchart` that declares nothing, which is a parse error wherever it lands. **Copy as Mermaid Diagram** is kept and is now the narrower job: the drawing alone, for a tool that wants only that. 14 tests on the two builders; verified live 2026-09-17 — David's 38-box graph copied as ~12.5KB and drew heading, summary, diagram, then tables in the app's own markdown preview. No row-count change.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-17d** (**new RD-121: the connection graph copies as Markdown or as a Mermaid diagram — and the app’s clipboard was broken.** Asked for by the user with the spot circled in a picture: _in name/location-name lookup graph preview, I want a copy icon, when click the icon it show contextmenu of `Copy as Markdown` and `Copy as Mermaid Diagram`, then implement those 2 copy format for me. for Mermaid, chose the most proper one for me._ One pure serializer (`graphTextExportHelpers.ts`, no React/DOM/app imports) writes both off the SAME visible node and edge lists `buildGraphSvg` uses. Markdown: a records table with the centre marked, connections grouped by relation with the gendered word, references capped at 12 a record. Mermaid: `flowchart LR` — the only diagram kind that carries this graph whole (arrows where a relation has two different ends, plain lines where not, a label on every line, arbitrary cross-links; a `mindmap` cannot link two branches, an `erDiagram` describes kinds rather than records) — with a `classDef` per record kind carrying the canvas’s own accent as a stroke. Two choices came out of the LIVE run rather than the design: references are capped (19KB → 7.6KB for David’s 29 boxes; Solomon alone cites 174) and the direction is `LR` (the same graph as `TD` renders as one unreadable row 28 boxes wide). **The verification also found the feature’s own blocker, an app-wide bug**: Electron exposes `clipboard` to the MAIN process only, so `systemUtils.copyToClipboard` in the renderer threw `Cannot read properties of undefined (reading 'writeText')` and every Copy in the app answered **Reload is needed**; it now sends `main:app:copy-to-clipboard` and main writes the clipboard. 11 unit tests on the two builders; verified live 2026-09-17 on the dev reader, both formats pasted into a `.md` and rendered in the app’s own markdown preview. Total 830 → **831**.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-17c** (**new RD-120: files can be copied INTO a Resources folder; RD-87 amended.** Asked for by the user with a picture of a group's menu circled: _as a user I want to be able to copy one or more files to the resources selected folder… in `Resource` for contextmenu of selected folder add `Add Files` to select files to copy to the folder._ A group's menu gains **Add Files** (`bi-file-earmark-plus`) under **Add Folder**, opening the multi-select picker; `copyFilesIntoResourcesFolder` then copies each picked file into that folder one at a time (sequential, `fs.copyFile`, the sources untouched), taking the next free name (`genFreeFilePath`, the index BEFORE the extension) rather than overwriting, passing over a path that is already that file here or is gone, and on a disk error sweeping up the part file and stopping with what already landed. The box invalidates its own cached walk, re-scans and opens if it was folded, and the toast counts what landed and what did not — plus, when nothing copied will be DRAWN (named after no open chapter, **Others** unticked), one sentence saying to tick **Others**, since a shelf that does not visibly change is indistinguishable from a copy that failed. Unit-tested on the real disk (the copy, a taken name, a file already there, a gone path, a folder, a refusal, a failure half way) and the wording and the `isNoneListed` handoff tested in the box. Verified live 2026-09-17 on the dev reader over CDP: the item sits between **Add Folder** and **Reveal in File Explorer** on the `Document` group; a `GEN.1.claude-check.txt` picked through the real dialog appeared under `GEN.1.*` without a refresh, the same file picked again landed as `GEN.1.claude-check (1).txt` with the toast **Add Files — 1 file copied** and the first copy untouched, and both were deleted and the group refreshed. Total 829 → **830**.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-17b** (**RD-118 and RD-119 amended: a note file lists its highlights and comments, and the markdown preview draws the file's own HTML, sanitized.** Asked for by the user the same day: with a picture of the Bible Notes panel, _it should have all like highlight and comments as well_; and with the README selected, _the markdown preview should be rendered_. `parseResourceNoteItems` now keeps a verse-mark item as a `verse` row (its key, bible key, open state and each mark's id, words and colour or comment — never the offsets), `ResourcesFileNotesComp` (now lazily loaded by the row) draws it with the Bible Notes panel's own mark line, extracted to `VerseAnnotationLineComp`, and a mark press opens the verse through `openVerseBibleItem`; nothing writes. markdown-it runs with `html: true` and EVERYTHING it outputs goes through DOMPurify (new direct dependency, already installed under mermaid): HTML profile only, no `style`/`form`/`input`/`button`/`textarea`/`select`/`option`/`dialog`/`template`, no `srcset`, no `data-*` but the diagram placeholder, a `style` only as a column's `text-align`, classes only `language-*`/`app-markdown-*`, no id or name that is `root` or starts `app-`/`dapp-`, and every `<img src>` — markdown or HTML — resolved beside the file. Verified live 2026-09-17 on the dev reader: `GEN.1.own` listed its four notes and three marked verses with their marks, a verse folded to a count, a mark opened Genesis 4:1 as another view, and the file's MD5 and timestamp were unchanged; the repository's `README.md` rendered badges, a centred screenshot from a path with spaces, a table of contents whose links scrolled, a table and a closed `<details>`, with only Electron's standard dev warnings on the console. Total unchanged at **829**.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-17a** (**new RD-118 and RD-119: a markdown file and a bible note file open inside the app from Resources.** Asked for by the user with two pictures — a `GEN.1.md` and a `GEN.1.own` circled in a Resources group, and the Bible Notes panel: _Resources should support in app preview for markdown file and Bible-note file… open bible-note preview should be read-only, open markdown-file should popup like bible-note and render the markdown. If possible add Mermaid diagram support._ A `.md` / `.markdown` row (now `bi-markdown`) opens a new popup page, `markdownPreview.html` (`src/markdown-preview/*`), keyed by the file's full path so a second press focuses the open window: markdown-it with raw HTML OFF, GitHub heading anchors (ids prefixed `md-heading-`), pictures resolved beside the file, links classified in one place (a heading scrolls, a `.md` opens in the same window with Back, http(s) goes to the browser, any other file is REVEALED, never run), ` ```mermaid ` blocks drawn by `mermaid` 12 (new dependency, imported only by a file that has one; `securityLevel: 'strict'`), a watcher that re-renders on save, and the page is on the renderer lockdown list beside the chatbot and AI Chat. A `.own` row gains a chevron and, only when opened, reads the file (size-capped at 16 MB) into its notes' ids and titles — verse-mark items left out; a note opens `bibleNote.html?preview-file=<path>&id=<n>`, the existing editor with `isReadOnly` set (the package shows its lock as locked) and `saveData` refusing on its own, no Bible Lookup insert button, _(Read-only)_ in the title. Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-16a** (**new RD-117: a Resources folder can be copied into the data folder; RD-87 amended.** Asked for by the user with a picture of a Resources group's menu, **Reveal in File Explorer** underlined: _move selected folder in Resources to under selected-dir so I can manage it easily_, then _after copied then change the selection to the copied one_. The group menu gains **Copy to Data Directory** (`bi-copy`) between Reveal and Remove Folder, left out for a folder already under `<parent dir>/resources`. It confirms with the source path and the destination, copies the tree one entry at a time (`fs.copyFile`, links skipped) under a hidden `.copying-<ms>-<name>` staging folder renamed into place at the end, and replaces the group IN PLACE in `resources-folder-list`, carrying its open/collapsed setting to the copy and deleting the original's.
