Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-15c** (**ED-27 and ST-21 amended: the weight select lists the family's real weights.** Asked for by the user with a picture of the Slide Editor's FONT box and the research in `extra-work/font`: the npm `font-list` package named families only, so the main process answered every family with `[]` and the weight select never rendered anywhere. `font-list` is removed; `electron/fontListHelpers.ts` lists families with their OpenType weights in one child process per app run, shared by asks that arrive while it runs (WPF `GetTypefaces()` with the faces it would merely SIMULATE dropped — `FamilyTypefaces` gave 113 of 240 families, `Moul` and `Khmer OS Battambang` among them, a bold they do not ship — plus the per-user font folder on Windows, `NSFontManager` through JXA with a `system_profiler` fallback on macOS, `fc-list` on Linux). The select sits beside the family, reads `Default` / `400 Regular` / `700 Bold`…, shows when the family ships more than one weight or a weight is already set, and keeps a saved weight the family lacks as `(Missing)`. Verified live 2026-09-15 on the dev Slide Editor (Windows): Arial offered Default, 400 Regular, 700 Bold and 900 Black; 700 Bold restyled the box and its thumbnail; Undo restored it. Not driven live: macOS and Linux enumeration (parsers unit-tested only) and the Settings and Foreground cards, which mount the same component. Total unchanged.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-15b** (**new RD-116: a Resources box marked Others lists the files named after no chapter.** Asked for by the user with a picture of the panel: _I want to be able to see others non related to `<book>.<chapter>.*` files so I don't have to do search for those files_. A picture or a handout kept beside the chapter files was reachable only by typing part of its name into the search. A checkbox **Others** now sits between the pattern chips and the magnifier; ticked (persisted panel-wide as `resources-others-showing`), every folder lists, under an **Others** heading after its chapter matches and any search hits, each file whose name is not `<book key>.<chapter>.*` for a book any bible model knows — collected in the same walk, so it reads no extra folder — capped at 200 with **Too many other files**. Verified live 2026-09-15 on the dev reader over CDP: ticking it listed a general folder's files under **Others** while the two chapter-named folders beside it stayed exactly as they were, no console errors or warnings, and unticking it restored every list. W-37 step 5 says so.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-15a** (**RD-84, RD-89, RD-114 amended: Resources reads a folder and two levels of folders under it, not eight.** Asked for by the user with a picture of the panel: a group for their whole home folder said **No matching files** over **Too many folders to search** — _make sure do 2 to 3 top level searching_. `MAX_SCAN_DEPTH` 8 → 2. Measured on that home folder with the walk's own rules (breadth-first, dot names skipped, 1500-folder budget): 64 folders read and complete at 2, 335 at 3, and at 4 or more the whole budget spent with nothing found. Stopping at the depth is not a truncation and draws no warning; the budgets stay as the backstop for a drive root. Verified live 2026-09-15 on the dev reader: the home-folder group reads **No matching files** with no warning, and the two library groups list the same `GEN.4.pdf`, `GEN.0.pdf` and `GEN.0.json` links as before. Total unchanged.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-14d** (**CB-68 extended: a link a site opens needs a press, and a WebSocket is held to the wall.** Security work, not a report (`AC-11`, `AC-15`). Measured from inside a live claude.ai guest: `ws://` to 127.0.0.1, localhost, 127.1, `[::1]` and 127.0.0.2 all opened with the handshake reaching a loopback server, because `*://` in a request filter is http(s) only; and in a harness on the app's own Electron, a page's `window.open` from a timer reached the window-open handler, which opens the system browser. Now the filter also names `ws://*/*` and `wss://*/*`, and the browser gets a page only within five seconds of a press in that guest, a refused one said on the window's own line. Verified live 2026-09-14 on the dev window: all five WebSockets closed 1006 with nothing received, two public `wss://` echoes still opened, and the probe's no-press window was handed nothing with the line naming example.com; 43 checks held.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-14c** (**CB-68 amended: a site in the AI Chat window may ASK for the microphone.** Reported by the user with a picture of claude.ai's dictation button under _Microphone access is blocked_ — the site's own advice points at a browser address bar the guest does not have, so the person had nothing to press. Decided with the user: ask per site. An audio-only request from a site's own https top page goes to the window (`askAiChatMicrophone`), which refuses it silently unless it is the tab in front on that tab's own site and otherwise asks on an amber line with **Don't allow** focused; a yes is per origin, in memory, until the app closes or Sign out; the camera, alone or with the microphone, stays refused; the permission check answers yes for that page because Electron's check has no "ask me" state. Verified live 2026-09-14 on the dev window over raw CDP into the guest: Claude's own **Dictate** raised the line; **Don't allow**, Escape and a tab switch each gave the page `NotAllowedError`; **Allow** gave a live audio track and the next ask no line; the same page behind another tab was refused on that yes; `probe-aichat.mjs` 91/91 over three sites (35 per site, was 33). Not driven live: Sign out taking the yes back, which would have signed the dev window out of three sites (unit-tested). Total unchanged.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-14b** (**new CB-72: the assistant changes what the user keeps, and every change can be put back; CB-42 amended: a delete moves the file to the trash.** Asked for by the user in three messages: CRUD tools for saved Bible passages, Bible notes, songs and slide documents; slides with their text and style; and _make sure all actions have backup action, e.g. delete it should move to trash and can undo_. Verified live 2026-09-14 on the dev app through fresh servers: 36 checks across songs, slides, Bible items and notes, all holding after the editing-history read-cache fix (MC-25); `owa_undo` bringing back a trashed slide document with its unsaved slides; and a picture of a document the tools created, ringed in the Documents list, then trashed. Not driven live: the refusal while a note is open in its own window, a Bible item re-pointed by version alone, renaming a list or a notes file, and the removal budget, which is unit-tested (MC-28).)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-14a** (**CB-08 amended: a provider with no key is pickable, and picking it opens its key box.** Asked for by the user with a picture of the head row's assistant list open on **Kimi — needs an API key**, greyed out: _if no api key for the api then still allow clickable but when click should bring to setting to the input text_. The row is a plain `<option>` marked `data-needs-key` in the muted colour; picking it leaves the tab on its provider and opens Settings → Others with the cursor in that provider's key box, through a 30-second setting read on mount and on focus — Settings is one window found again by its URL, so a parameter would open a second. An arrow on the closed list steps over the row as it did a disabled one. Verified live 2026-09-14 on the dev app over raw CDP: a new Settings window had the cursor in **Kimi API Key** ~0.9 s after the pick; with Settings open on **General** and the help window in front, the same window came forward, switched to Others and focused the box in ~0.2 s, one Settings window throughout; the tab and `chatbot-llm-provider` stayed on `anthropic`. The arrow step-over is unit-tested (`findSteppedProvider`), not driven live: a real arrow press switches providers and rewrites the stored default.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-12k** (**PM-48 amended: the missing-fonts banner carries the rebuild.** Asked for by the user with a picture of **Refresh PPTX Slides** circled in a document's context menu and an arrow to the yellow banner: _so I can refresh after installed new fonts_. A PPTX or DOCX records its missing fonts in `info.json` when its preview is BUILT, so installing one changed nothing on screen until the rebuild — which lived only in the Documents row's right-click menu. The banner's header row now carries **Refresh PPTX Slides** (a PPTX) or **Refresh DOCX Pages** (a DOCX), collapsed or expanded, the same `removePptxHtmlsPreview` + update event as the menu item; a slide document gets no button, since it has nothing to rebuild. The converter makes a new instance and rescans the font folders on every run, so no restart is needed. Verified live 2026-09-12 on the dev Presenter: pressing it on a Khmer PPTX rebuilt the slides and the banner went from **Missing fonts (5)** to **(2)**. Total unchanged.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-12j** (**PM-29 amended: a colour swatch is a button, and choosing one keeps the keyboard on it.** Found by the 2026-09-12 robot run. The row still said the swatches were `role=group`, the robot-test notes said plain `div`s carrying only a title, and both were stale: `RenderColorComp` / `RenderNoColorComp` are `role="button"` named by the colour, with `aria-pressed` on the one in force, and Enter/Space press them. What the run did find was the focus. Every choice remounted the picker, whose key carried the colour, so a keyboard user was dropped out of the row after each press. The picker is keyed by SCREEN now, and puts focus back on the pressed swatch if a re-render drops it within 2 s of the press. Verified live 2026-09-12 on the dev Presenter: navy by mouse, then Tab + Enter onto blue, then No Color, and each left `focused pressed` on the swatch just pressed. Total unchanged.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-12i** (**CB-09 amended: on a Mac, a walkthrough minimises the help window and nothing else.** Reported by the user with a picture from macOS: pressing **Do it for me** minimised every window, the presenter included. The help window is opened as a child of the app window, and macOS cannot minimise a child window on its own — it minimised the parent and every other popup on it, left the help window up, and closing the card brought nothing back. Measured live 2026-09-12 on the dev app before the fix, off the OS window list: presenter and Settings off screen, help window on. After it, the help window alone goes to the Dock (~1 s), the presenter stays on screen throughout, and the stop brings the help window back (~0.6 s). Windows is untouched. Total unchanged.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-12h** (**CB-36 amended: the keyless assistant answers again, from Kilo Code alone.** Reported by the user with a picture of Free answering _I could not answer that: The assistant service answered 500_. Two faults under one line: LLM7, Free's default service, had gone paid under it — `gpt-oss` answered `model_unavailable`, so every keyless question fell to the offline guide — and the 500 itself was the app's own tool host, which the window printed as a status code. The **Model** list is now Nemotron Lightning, Nex Pro and Step Flash, each measured through the real tool loop; a tab saved on a dropped free model is put back on the first choice; the notice names Kilo Code alone; and a tool-host failure says _The app's own help service did not respond…_ instead of a number or the provider's name.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-12g** (**new CB-71: every asset in a conversation opens, and every preview downloads.** Asked for by the user: _as a user I want to always be able to download assets in chat session. Make sure all assets: files/images clickable to see preview and on preview has download icon to download the asset._ A conversation accumulates assets — the user's own screenshot, a dropped file, the report just written, the song just created — and what a chip DID depended on which kind it was: a picture opened big, everything else opened a file-manager window BEHIND the app. So the only asset a volunteer could look at was one they had usually just made themselves, and the only way to keep one was to go hunting in Explorer. Now one overlay shows all of them (picture, scrollable text, or a card naming what cannot be drawn) with **Download**, **Copy** and **Open folder** under it. Content is read only on opening and dropped on closing; `fsGetFileSize` is asked FIRST, so a picture over 8 MB or a text file over 512 KB is measured and named rather than read; a file already in Downloads is revealed, never copied twice. A test caught the defect worth keeping: "is this text?" must never be decided by `kind`, because every file an answer offers arrives typed `text` — a `.mp4` would have been read as UTF-8. Verified live 2026-09-12 on the dev Reader: the report's `.md` opened as readable text, its `.png` opened as a picture read off the disk, and Download on a file already in Downloads left two files rather than three. Total 826 -> **827**.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-12f** (**CB-32 amended: a report says WHO wants it, and hands over the address and the text.** Asked for by the user with a picture of the Report button under a box reading _font-family is too small_ — _find email address … then provide copy-able content and copy-able email address_ — and, mid-change, _app auto email sometime out-of-date, use the found email from help page as primary and from the app as secondary_. The Send press used to end on _saved into Downloads, pass it on however you like_: a file and no idea who wanted it. Now the answer names the maintainers' address and a `[Open Worship app] <title> (<reference>)` subject, offers **Copy report** / **Copy subject** / **Copy picture** / **Copy email address** / **Email it** (and every picture chip in the window carries a clipboard icon, the preview's Copy included), and the saved document opens with a **How to send this** section; the address is read off the LIVE help page through the same locked-down reader `owa_read_website` uses — which on 2026-09-12 named `info@openworship.app` while `package.json` still said `owf2025@gmail.com`, the staleness the user meant — with the package as the fallback, and both places say which it was. The document also carries the machine, the selection and run sheet, each screen's content, the displays, 20 console lines and who investigated. Verified live 2026-09-12 on the dev Reader with Claude Sonnet 5: two reports (≈ $0.10 each), all four Copy buttons answering in the transcript, the picture chip's icon flipping to a tick in the ask row and under an answer without opening the preview, and the window reopened reading the help page's address; **Email it** was not pressed (it opens the operator's mail client) and is covered by its unit test. Total unchanged at **826**.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-12e** (**new RD-115: a `.json` beside the verse is a list of clickable links.** Asked for by the user with a picture of their own Resources panel — a `GEN.0.json` drawn with the "unknown file" diamond, and a library of `<BOOK>.0.json` files behind it holding a title and a YouTube address per video. The default schema is the one their files already had (an array of `{title, url}`, a bare address allowed as shorthand, a blank title falling back to its own address), documented in `docs/schemas/resource-links.schema.json` and implemented once in `resourceLinksHelpers.ts`. **The extension only makes a row a CANDIDATE**: `.json` is a general-purpose format, so the content decides — anything that does not yield at least one openable link stays an ordinary file row, `bi-filetype-json`, no chevron, opened by the OS, with no warning to dismiss. `http(s)` only, because `openExternalURL` is `shell.openExternal` and every desktop will launch whatever program has claimed some other scheme; a dropped entry is COUNTED so a typo shows. Read on demand — as its row appears for a verse match, on the first click for a search hit, so a one-letter search is not 200 file reads — and let go of when the row folds. Verified live 2026-09-12 on the dev Reader: their own `GEN.0.json` listed three Khmer video titles, a probe with a bad entry showed its two good rows and **1 entry was not understood**, a probe holding ordinary data stayed a file row (`expandable` absent from the a11y tree), and a press on a title opened the video in Chrome. Total 825 -> **826**.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-12d** (**new RD-114: a Resources folder can be added by dropping it.** Asked for by the user with a picture of the panel: _as a user I want to do drag/drop folder to do folder adding_. The only ways in were the **Add Folder** button and the picker dialog behind it — the long way round when the folder is already open in Explorer. The view now takes a dropped folder anywhere on itself, says **Drop folders here** in its own toolbar row while one is held over it, and refuses a dropped FILE in words rather than quietly shelving the folder it sits in (as often Downloads as a library, walked 8 levels deep). Verified live 2026-09-12 on the dev reader over CDP: the drop listed `GEN.4.pdf` and book-level `GEN.0.notes.pdf` under Genesis 4, a second drop of the same folder said **Folder is already added**, a dropped `.pdf` shelved nothing, and the test folder was removed again through the panel's own **Remove Folder**. W-37 step 3 says so.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-12c** (**new CB-70: opening either AI window asks the caution FIRST.** Asked for by the user with a picture of the toolbar, both AI icons circled: _when I click the icon I want to see the confirm message of the warning about AI cautious first, then confirm to open_ — both buttons, every press. CB-69's caution is read once the window is already up, by somebody who has decided to ask; this is the decision itself, and the ✨ AI Chat window had no caution anywhere at all. One confirm now gates every user-initiated route into either window — the two toolbar buttons, the two Tools entries including Ctrl+Shift+A, and the two native Help menu items — because a caution a menu item walks around is a caution nobody is given. Two of its three sentences are shared and one is not: the assistant reads THIS app and can offer a press that reaches a live projector, the ✨ is a stranger's website where the words leave the machine, and one warning vague enough to cover both would have warned about neither. It FAILS OPEN where no dialog can be drawn (Local Web Share, the Lyric Editor), since `showAppConfirm` answering `false` for a missing popup host is indistinguishable from Cancel and would have made Ctrl+Shift+A silently do nothing. On the 🤖 the master switch is still asked first. Verified live 2026-09-12 on the dev Reader: both wordings read back off the real dialog, **Open** opened the chatbot window and **Cancel** on the ✨ left the AI Chat window unopened. Total 824 -> **825**.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-12b** (**CB-68 extended: the AI Chat box got a fifth wall, and a way to sign out.** Security work on the one renderer in this app that runs a page nobody here wrote. Measured from inside a live guest on claude.ai: the site could not READ anything on this computer — CORS refused the app's CDP door, `host.mjs`'s Origin check refused its MCP door, Chromium's own origin rule refused a CDP WebSocket — but a blind `no-cors` fetch at BOTH doors went out and was served, and the same request reaches the church's router, NAS and printers. The guest session now cancels every request to a local or private address, by the same `webUrlPolicy.mjs` predicate the MCP firewall uses (`checkIsLocalHostname`, added there and now the one implementation `checkWebUrl` uses too); after it, both doors, `127.1`, `2130706433`, `0x7f.1`, `127.0.0.2`, `[::1]`, `192.168.1.1`, `169.254.169.254` and `printer` are all refused and the site's own origin still answers 200 — the positive control matters, a wall that blocks the site is a brick. Beside it, `AC-01`: the persistent partition is a credential store, and closing every tab never touched it, so **Sign out of every site** is now a **↤** in the head row and a line on the chooser card, asking first with _Keep me signed in_ focused, clearing the tabs' remembered pages and site-given names while keeping a name the user typed. `probe-aichat.mjs` 22 → **33 checks, all held**. Verified live 2026-09-12 on the dev window. Total 824 (no new row).)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-12a** (**CB-31 amended and new CB-69: a control the user POINTED AT could not be traced back, and nothing in the window cautioned against trusting an answer.** Reported with a picture: a **Bible View** chip on the ask row of the Bible Reader, and pressing it answering _There is nothing left to show for that one_ — a sentence true of the code and false of the window, where the pane was in plain sight. `selectorOf` prefers a name over a position because a name survives a re-render, but never asked whether the name named ONE thing: the Reader routinely shows two panes carrying `data-widget-name="Bible View"` as immediate siblings, and measured live their candidate chains were byte-identical at all 8 steps, every one matching 2 elements — walking up can never rescue that, because every ancestor they share is the same node. The cost was never the pane: one ambiguous ancestor makes every descendant untraceable too, **156 of 949 elements in that window had no selector at all**. A named part with a twin among its siblings now keeps the name AND says which one (156 → 12 nulls, 144 fixed, 0 broken, 0 mis-targeted), a lone one is left clean, and a chip whose selector still cannot be built falls back to the words on it. Beside it, at the user’s ask — _add a cautious warning message somewhere about AI bad impact and be carefull while using it_ — the empty state gained a standing caution about whether an answer is RIGHT, which nothing in the window had ever said (the one warning there is about where the words GO, on the keyless provider only). Verified live 2026-09-12 on the dev Reader: both panes ring distinctly (`y: 41` and `y: 440`), and the note read back off the reopened help window. Total 823 -> **824**.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-11f** (**new RD-113: the in-verse underlines follow the BIBLE, so a Khmer verse gets them too.** Reported with a picture of the reader on Genesis 4 in two columns — the KJV one underlining Adam / Eve / Cain / Abel, the Khmer one beside it underlining nothing. The dataset is English, the FEATURE need not be: a third derived file, `verse-text-needles-<code>.json` (85KB for `km`, one entry per interned id), carries that language’s own spellings and a new matcher runs the opposite way round from the English one — candidates come from the verse’s (then the chapter’s) evidence and are looked FOR in the text, because Khmer has neither word boundaries to cut on nor the capital letter that carries most of the English matcher’s precision. No dataset-wide tier at all: measured over the whole shipped Khmer bible it would have added 824 matches including “the Jews”, “wild”, “riches” and “lord” as people. RD-65, RD-66, RD-80 and RD-91 updated with it. Verified live 2026-09-11 on the dev reader. )

Let me look for the countdown controls."_ with nothing started. Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-09d** (**new CB-57: the walkthrough can DO the panels recipe from the divider, and a divider is a thing the assistant can find.** Reported with a screenshot: the W-31 card on step 1/5 saying _"Open the View menu at the very top"_ beside the user's own right-click on a panel divider, open on Reset Size / Close First Widget / Close Second Widget. The recipe knew only the native View menu, which no card can press and a popup window does not have; the divider had no name at all (`owa_find_ui "Reset Size"` → 0); and the View-menu step itself was dropped as a go-to-this-window step because its example list says _presenter_. Now every divider is named `Divider between A and B`, the card right-clicks it where it is and chooses the item on the next press, W-31 carries the divider route as steps 7–9, a pane NAMED with a kind noun (_Presenting Flow List_) is an exact match again (its collapsed strip was refused by `owa_click`), and the drop rule reads a step's first sentence only. Verified live 2026-09-09 through the app's own host with screenshots. Total 820 -> **821**.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-09c** (**new CB-56: the assistant knows what the user is in the MIDDLE of, and steps the slides.** No user report: the standing corpus passed 12/12 on Claude Sonnet 5, so the run asked the other half of rung 5 — what is SELECTED, with the selection deliberately different from what the projector held. It answered _which song is selected?_ with the song on the screen, not the highlighted one (the only document any tool had ever named), and _show the next slide_ ran to the ten-round cap — six `owa_list_ui` hunts for slide cards that had no accessible name, a 200-row dump (34 574 tokens into the cache), a press on a slide's `Index: 5` badge that changed the projector, then _"I could not find an answer for that"_ because the last round spent its whole 2 000-token budget thinking (every Claude Sonnet 5 round carries a `thinking` block by default). Now `owa_app_state` on the Presenter carries the selected document, its slides with their first words, the one on a screen and the arrow keys' next and previous, every slide card carries `aria-label` (`Slide 5: <name>`) so `owa_click` presents one by exact words, `/selected` / `/next` / `/previous` do it with no model, the offline bot answers from the same field, the Anthropic loop has a 6 000-token budget and an honest "ran out of room" fallback, and the top search hit says to open the page before writing steps (the prompt rule alone was ignored 4 of 4). The same asks: the selected song right in 2 rounds; _show the next slide_ 2 rounds and one confirmation, then one press and a check (4 rounds, 7.6 s, verified on the wall). Verified live 2026-09-09. Total 819 -> **820**.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-09b** (**new CB-55: the assistant knows what is ON the projector, and says it.** No user report: the standing corpus passed 12/12 on Claude Sonnet 5, so the run followed the panic shapes THROUGH — pressed the "yes" under them and re-asked them with the projector actually showing a Khmer verse. It answered _"nothing has actually been sent to it yet: turning the screen on just gives you a blank canvas"_ after 9 rounds and 36 s, _"yes, the screen is on"_ with not a word about what, and its "yes" took 8 rounds, a wrong press (_0 Screen: 0_) and a doubled label — because `owa_list_screens` said whether a screen was on and nothing about what it held. Now it answers what each screen holds (the slide with its first words, the passage, the background, the foreground widgets, the lock), the exact words on that screen's show/hide and Clear buttons, and where the Mini Screen panel sits; the same asks name the verse on the wall, the "yes" is 3 rounds, and the offline bot and `/screen` say the same with no model. Verified live 2026-09-09. Total 818 -> **819**.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-09** (**new CB-54: a song page's chords reach the file, whatever the model does with the page.** Reported by the user with a screenshot of a Khmer hymnal's chord page, its `|G` markers circled: _no key note imported_ for _Create a lyric file from_ that page. The saved transcript and the `.owl` on disk said why — Claude Sonnet 5 read the page, then handed the drafter its OWN copy of the words, every fragment rejoined and not one of the page's 36 chords kept, exactly as `EC-98` had recorded no provider hands a page over whole. Now the drafter takes the page ADDRESS (`owa_lyric_validate` with `url`) and reads the page itself through the same locked-down window, address check, budget and banner as `owa_read_website`, and the model never gets a turn in between: one tool call, two rounds, 36 chord marks on disk, the preview drawing `|G |G |Em` over the words as the site does. Handing the page over whole had also shown a chord site's toolbar drafted as Verse 1 (the `Key: G · Time: 4/4` strip is the boundary now; chordless rows above the first chord are swept and named) and the site's own `© 2026` footer filed as the hymn's copyright (a notice naming the site is skipped). Verified live 2026-09-09. Total 817 -> **818**.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-08d** (**new CB-53: the assistant offers a change to the projector and never presses it unasked, and a question costs a tenth of what it did.** No user report: the standing corpus re-asked on Claude Sonnet 5 with every round's token usage read off the wire. Asked _Nothing is showing on the projector_, the model rang the wrong control and then pressed F5 through chrome-devtools' `press_key` on two windows — the congregation's screen came on with nobody asking; asked _the words no come out big screen_, it took three page snapshots, read the console, ran to the ten-round cap and answered _I could not find an answer for that_ after 72 s and 225 000 tokens. Now the model is offered the app's own 19 tools and nothing of chrome-devtools' (29 withheld), the prompt names showing a screen and a shortcut key in the offered-never-done rule, `owa_click` refuses a loose match by name, a tool field name quoted as evidence is scrubbed, and the Anthropic loop is prompt-cached: 813 000 full-price input tokens for the twelve questions → 62 full-price plus 41 600 written and 401 600 read ($1.63 → $0.18). Verified live 2026-09-08. Total 816 -> **817**.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-08c** (**new CB-52: Do it presses the button the step names, and closes what is in the way first.** Reported with a screenshot — the Bible Lookup popup open over the Presenter and the walkthrough’s ring drawn THROUGH it onto a line of Genesis, _“does not work while modal present”_, then _“many question fail during Do it”_. Measured by a new harness that presses Do it through every step of every recipe: 224 presses, 124 refused, and at least ten of the 92 counted as done had pressed the WRONG control (the projector’s Clear All for the drawing panel’s Clear; the help window opened for a bolded ASSISTANT). Now a covered control is closed out of the way (popup, menu, floating panel; never a question the app asks), only an exactly-named control is pressed (a look-alike is refused with its name), a step that only describes what to see reads Next, a bold over 40 characters or of one character no longer eats the next label, and the four tour pages start a walkthrough. Verified live 2026-09-08. Total 815 -> **816**.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-08b** (**new CB-51: a drafted song is the only thing to press, with a key or without.** The four _Try asking_ chips graded on the user’s own assistant, Kimi K2.6 on Moonshot’s free tier: two pass clean; the two that end in a drafted song both carried model-written pills (_Create the file_, _Copy the text_) drawn brighter than the real buttons, and pressing one cost 4 rounds and an offer to overwrite; the paste the chip invites arrived inside the free tier’s minute, was refused, and the offline bot searched the manual for the hymn. Now the echo pills are dropped, the offline bot drafts a lyric paste itself (~2 s, no model), a hymnal text page handed over whole drafts its numbered stanzas with the page’s own `Title`/`Author`/`Copyright: Public Domain` (it was sixteen verses of menus), a taken name is refused with the free name spelt out, and Kimi no longer starts a walkthrough card on a plain how-do-I. Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-08a** (**new CB-50: a recipe id never reaches the answer.** Research, not a report: the standing corpus asked on Claude, every answer right, and the where-is one opened with _"W-08 has exactly what you need"_ — an id the prompt forbids by name, written from a search hit, and written AGAIN when asked after the fix. The ids are now scrubbed in code at both ends: out of every search excerpt (page bodies already were; excerpts were not) and out of the answer at the seam the `OPTIONS:` frame leaves by, replaced with the page's own title.
