Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-05b** (**new CB-49: a picked question is a label, not a search.** Measured on all 258 supported questions with a page: the search put that page first 57% of the time, so a chip or a suggestion — the app's own words — got the wrong page two times in five. The search now consults the corpus first and puts a filed page first, marked; the assistant is told the page on a picked question and opens it without searching; and a hit that merely shares a word (score under 6 — no right answer in 258 scored lower) is no longer an answer, so _Can it stream to Facebook?_ stops offering to walk the user through the Presenter overview. Held-out paraphrases unchanged. Verified live 2026-09-03. Total 808 -> **809**.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-05a** (**new CB-48: commands that need no assistant.** Asked for from the app — _"for some task the chatbot should not ask the llm api"_, _"add build action like `/presenter-screen-show`"_, _"so user don't have to ask llm"_. Measured first: on one afternoon three of the four assistants answered 429 within an hour, and the offline guide bot the window fell back to got 4 of the 12 standing questions right, answering "nothing is showing" with no way to change it. A line starting with `/` is now a command run through the app's own tools on the spot — the screen on and off, the five clears, find, goto, here, help — 0 model rounds, about 1.6 s, every answer read back after the press, and the offline bot's "nothing is showing" now carries a button that turns it on. Two offline-bot fixes rode along (a how-do-I that mentions the screen, and the window name as a search word). Verified live 2026-09-02. Total 807 -> **808**.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-04d** (**new CB-47, and CB-46 + CB-17 amended: the window says what it is DOING, walks its own tips, and shows the whole list of what it can be asked.** Three things asked for from the app, all about the same complaint -- the window does not say enough about itself. A question that reads a web page and drafts a song is most of a minute behind one unchanging line, `Looking it up... press Stop to give up on it.`, which cannot tell a window that is working from one that has hung: the wait is now NARRATED, one line per thing it actually does, in the user's words rather than a tool name (`Searching the guide for "background"`, `Reading example.com`, `Creating a new song: "Amazing Grace"`), finished steps kept above the running one so it reads as progress rather than as a spinner. The 💡 tip line gained **<** and **>**: pressing the sentence still gives a RANDOM one, but the arrows walk the list in order and wrap, which is the only way to see all of them and the only way back to one that changed while it was being read. And the empty window's four chips got **More...**, which opens every question the assistant is prepared for in this window -- 184 in the Presenter, grouped by the panel each belongs to -- because a help window whose scope has to be guessed at gets used for the one thing somebody once saw it do. Verified live 2026-09-04 on the presenter with Claude Sonnet 5. Total 806 -> **807**.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-04c** (**CB-45 rewritten -- the chords are part of the song now.** Reported from the app with the file the assistant had just made: a verse drafted as eleven lines, each one a fragment with `|D` sitting in front of it as text somebody sings. CB-45 was measured against a page that flattened `|` and `D` onto separate lines; most chord sites print `|D` -- one token, glued to the syllable it lands on -- which matched neither the bar pattern nor open-lyric's chord pattern, so every chord read as an ordinary WORD, and a bare word line following a bare word line is exactly what starts a new line, so the row never joined either. The chord is now written WHERE IT LANDS, as `|[D]` with the bar OUTSIDE the brackets -- `[|D]` is what the page looks like and it is refused by open-lyric and by our own validator alike, both probed before the form was chosen and the probe kept as a test -- and bounded three ways so it cannot eat a leading word that happens to be a chord. Two more from the same page: a region carries the chords that OPEN it without COUNTING them, and nothing printed under a credit line is a translation of it. Total unchanged at 806.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-04b** (**new CB-46: the ask box remembers what it has been asked, and the window says what it can do.** Asked for from the app with a screenshot: _"chatbot, it should remember asking history, when editing the alt+arrow-up/down should get the text from history"_, then _"show this kind of tip somewhere to let user know any features"_. Retyping a question that was nearly right — the wrong verse reference, the wrong screen number — is the part of using this window that costs the most on a machine in a back room minutes before a service. **Alt+↑** / **Alt+↓** now walk it, terminal-style, over one list for the whole window that outlives the tab it was typed in and the day it was typed on, with the half-written question held aside and handed back at the bottom of the walk. The plain arrows stay with the cursor and the suggestion list, which is why the recall wants **Alt**. The second half is discovery: the camera, the pointer, **Report**, the walkthrough cards and the recall itself are all built and none of them announces itself, so one 💡 line above the box names one at a time, randomly, never the same twice running. Total 805 -> **806**.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-04a** (**new CB-45: a song PAGE is not a song.** `CB-43` shipped drafting from raw words, and the user's first real input was a LINK -- the case it was worst at. Measured on eight real song pages before anything was written: every one produced a valid document and not one produced the right song. The menus, view counts, fretboard chart and footer were drafted as verses; the song's own labels were swallowed, so a four-verse hymn came out as one undivided `Verse`; and every sung line was broken into the fragments the chord columns cut it into. `tools/owa-devtools-mcp/lyricPageText.mjs` reads a page into the lines of a song -- region by walls of wordless lines scored on CHORDS first, fragments rejoined with nothing between them, a fragment after a fragment starting a new line, a label never joined to anything -- with a second reader, keyed on line length, for a hymn-text site that has no chords at all. After: 14/14 real pages valid, with structures matching the page's own printed play order. `owa_read_website`'s screenshot became whole-page (it was a 768px viewport) and its page read gained a timeout that was leaking hidden windows. Total 804 -> **805**.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-03b** (**new CB-44: the assistant no longer reports an outcome it did not see.** Reported from the app with a screenshot: asked _"How do I show a screen?"_, it answered correctly, offered to do it, was told _"Yes, turn it on"_ — and replied _"Done — the screen is now showing."_ with nothing on the wall. Two faults, one under the other. The presenter carries FOUR toolbar-reveal decorations titled **Show**, one per auto-hide footer; `owa_click` ranks an exact label above every looser fit, so the bare word beat the screen's own `Toggle showing screen` control every time and the press landed on a decoration. And `owa_click` answered with what it had PRESSED, never with what the press DID, so a model that managed to click something reported the goal achieved. The decorations are now **Reveal Hidden Controls** — the word _show_ is gone from them, because renaming to _Show Hidden Controls_ still won the bare word on a whole-word match — and `owa_click` reads the control back after the press: `isOnNow` for a control with a state, `didChange`, and an `unverified` sentence when the control came out of it untouched. The screen toggle's title went through `tran()` on the way (it was hardcoded English, so a Khmer window's most important control answered to nothing), and `owa_list_screens` — the tool the prompt names as the FIRST thing to look at — stopped returning ~2.8 KB of Electron display metadata to say _no_. Total 804 → **805**.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-03a** (**new CB-43: the assistant turns words a volunteer already has into a song file.** Lyrics arrive pasted, on a page, or in a `.txt` on a memory stick, and until now the only routes in were an account, a 36-hymn catalogue, or typing Open Lyric notation by hand. The model cannot write that notation: measured against the real validator, a plausible attempt makes 9 mistakes and a CAREFUL one still makes 2 — `CC` where `Cx2` is required, and free text inside `Instrumental`, which takes chords only — and both are invisible from the outside, so no prompt fixes them. `owa_lyric_validate` gained `mode: "draft"` (+61 tokens a round, against ~450 for a tool of its own), which writes the notation and round-trips it through the validator so it cannot ship a broken song. The chat never shows the notation: two buttons appear under the answer, **Create "<title>"** and **Copy song text**. The Presenter's **Try asking** chips now offer it, in place of _Where is the clear button?_. Total 803 → **804**.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-02e** (**new CB-39: a walkthrough happens in the window the task happens in, so the ring has the right controls to choose from.** Reported with a screenshot: a card headed _Settings: language, theme, fonts, folders_ drawn in the **Presenter**, red ring around a Bible version button — _"this wrong highlight, and also wrong page. it should help user open setting page and assist user in setting page."_ The model started the recipe with no page, so it ran in the main window; its step names nine bold words, seven of which exist only in Settings, and the candidate list fell through to `English` — out of _"Language: click English"_ — which is an exact whole word of the button labelled `KJV English KJV`. `owa_find_ui "English"` answers **3 identical matches in the Presenter and 0 in Settings**, so no matcher could have saved that ring: the card was in the wrong window. A recipe now says which window it is about, read out of its own opening step, and that wins over the page the caller named; measured over all 44 documents, 5 name exactly one window and all 5 are right, 5 name several and are left alone, 29 name none. Internal recipe ids stopped being offered to the card as controls to ring. Total 802 → **803**.)
Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-02d** (**new CB-38: a walkthrough of a window that is not open now OPENS it, instead of reporting a fault.** Reported from the app with a screenshot: asking about Settings, the user got a correct answer, pressed **Do it for me**, and was handed the tool’s own words — _"That did not work: The app has no open page matching \"setting.html\". The open pages are: chatbot.html?uuid=chatbot, presenter.html."_ — for a window that was one press of the ⚙️ button away. The chatbot held a hand-written map of TWO of the eight windows beside `botFocus.mjs`, which declares all eight; the two it knew, it only told about; and the generic catch printed `error.message` for every failed button press there will ever be. The window is now opened — `owa_goto_page` for a page the main window navigates to, a press of the new `openFind` control for a window of its own — and the walkthrough starts in it, once, never in a loop. `owa_goto_page` also stopped refusing the Document Editor its own system prompt promised. Total 801 → **802**.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-02c** (**new CB-37: the MCP tools an agent drives this app with now have a firewall.** Asked for from the app: _"enhance `owa-devtools-mcp`, add firewall on it. to prevent any harmfull activitiy"_, then _"let say security first"_ and _"update html\chatbot.html to prevent all harmfull activities like accessing file sytem api, reading secret, execute script without mcp tool"_. Every renderer runs with `nodeIntegration: true`, so `evaluate_script` over the MCP host was arbitrary code execution on the operator's machine — proven live, `require('os').userInfo()` and `require('fs').readFileSync(setting.json)`, from a script with no credential — and that tool was sent to the chatbot's model on every round, where an attachment somebody handed the user could reach it. `firewall.mjs` now sits on the one seam both doors share: three tools denied AND dropped from `tools/list` (47 → 44, ~668 tokens/round), navigation allowlisted to pages the app serves, a **point-don't-press** interlock on labels that cannot be undone, an acting-call rate limit, and provider keys scrubbed out of every result. The chatbot window separately loses `require` and `process.env` in the preload and gains its own CSP whose `connect-src` names the assistants it may speak to. Total 800 → **801**.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-02b** (**new CB-36: the app now has an assistant before anybody has an API key.** Asked for from `awesome-free-llm-apis`: _"is there any api we can use ... when user have no api keys set we should provide chatbot access as well"_, then _"show warning when using free one, let user aware of risk"_. Of 17 free providers only three answer keyless and only two can call a tool, which is the one thing this bot cannot work without; both are wired as one `Free` provider that sorts LAST so any real key still outranks it. Graded first against the standing corpus through the real 44-tool loop — 7/8 answered, 0 internals leaked — which is what sized the free-only round cap and found three defects a paid API never produces. The trade is stated in a sticky notice above the log and in Settings, never made quietly. Total 799 → **800**.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-02a** (**new CB-35: a picture with an empty box is a question, not a malformed request.** Reported from the window with the transcript: the assistant asked for a screenshot, the user sent one with nothing typed, and the provider refused the request outright because only a file or a pointed-at control contributes words — so the ask arrived as an empty text block. The refusal was then shown as an unreachable provider, with the offline bot's generic greeting under it. One decision point now says what the model is asked, the loop refuses to post an empty ask at all, and the offline half degrades honestly. Total 798 → **799**.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-01m** (**new CB-33, CB-34 and PC-33: the assistant and the annotation overlay belong to every window now.** Asked for from the app: _"add chatbot assistant and presenting-control to all pages except about.html, chatbot.html, finder.html, screen.html"_, then _"have to extend the list to support all question files"_. One component mounts both on all nine pages; the way in everywhere is **Tools → Start Controlling** / **Ctrl+Shift+P** and **Tools → App Assistant** / **Ctrl+Shift+A**, since only three windows have a top bar to hold a button. The menu click now routes to the FOCUSED window — one entry is kept per menu key, so the last window to load owned it and opening Settings quietly took _Start Controlling_ away from the presenter. **ASKING ABOUT** grew from two names to eight, declared once in `botFocus.mjs` and read by the picker, the two MCP tool schemas, the manual's focus filter and the session validator alike; the corpus grew `bible-note`, `web-editor` and `lyric-editor`, and the Slide Editor's own questions were split out of the Presenter's. CB-01 rewritten. Total 795 → **798**.)
Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-01l** (**new CB-32: the ask box holds several lines, and the help window can report a bug for you.** Asked for from the app with a screenshot of the ask row: _"change input text to textarea to support multiple lines"_, then _"under `Ask` add `Report`"_ — the agent to investigate first, collect the evidence and prepare it for an issue tracker — then _"ctrl+enter to ask, for normal enter just make new line"_ and _"to prevent click REPORT by accident, should confirm before running report"_. The box is a growing textarea; **Report** sits under **Ask**, asks before it runs, photographs the app, reads build/window/screens/console for itself, has the model investigate the live app, and prepares a document nothing sends until a second press. There is no issue tracker endpoint yet and the window says so rather than pretending: it saves the report and the picture into Downloads. Total 794 → **795**.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-01k** (**new CB-26..CB-30: the ask box takes more than words, and an answer on its way can be added to.** Asked for from the app: attach files and images, paste an image, point at a DOM element, take a screenshot — and, mid-turn, _"during waiting for api response user should able to add more input"_ and _"for something unclear the ai should ask for more input from user"_. Five ways in (paperclip, paste, drop, **📷**, **🎯**), all landing on one capped list of chips; the picture is held in memory for the life of the window and NEVER written to `chatbot-sessions`, which the app reads whole at startup — a reopened tab shows the chip greyed rather than a settings file with a screenshot in it. Pointing swallows the choosing click in the capture phase, so asking what **Clear Bible** does never clears the bible, and answers with a unique selector tested against the document before it is returned. The box is no longer disabled while an answer comes: **Add** folds what you type into the answer being written, a **Stop** puts every added word back in the box, and anything that arrives too late to fold is asked on its own and says so. The assistant can ask to be SHOWN — a `NEEDS:` frame the code strips unconditionally, rendered as the same buttons. The Presenting Control gains a **📷** that photographs the app with the drawing on it, offering ask / copy / save-into-images, over the one capture IPC that also serves the new `owa_screenshot`. Two tools added to the server and NEITHER offered to the chatbot's model, so a volunteer's question costs the same tokens per round as before. Total 788 → **794**.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-01j** (**CB-02, CB-08 and CB-12 amended: every head-row drop-down says what it is FOR, not only what it is set to.** Reported from the app with a screenshot circling the pair — the head row read `Presenter | ChatGPT`, two words with nothing to say which was the half of the app and which the service answering about it. The only explanation was a `title` hover, which is not a thing a volunteer goes looking for, and an `aria-label` no sighted user ever meets. Each control now wears a visible caption — **ASKING ABOUT**, **ASSISTANT**, **MODEL**, and **ANSWERS FROM** over the offline button — beside it, as a real `<label>` wrapping the control, so the pair reads as a line and the caption is a second click target for the control it names. Below 560px, the width this window opens at, the caption moves over its control instead: three captions written beside three controls is 463px of a 436px row, and the thing that would be pushed off the end is the model name. Verified live 2026-09-01 at both widths. No `aria-label` moved and no class was renamed, so `extra-work/verify-chatbot-e2e.mjs`, which finds all three by `.chat-head-row select[aria-label]`, is untouched. Total 788.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-01i** (**new CB-24 and CB-25: the answer, and the walkthrough under it, are about the question that was asked.** Reported from the app with a screenshot and in the user's own words -- _"get many wrong answers. the assistant help with unrelevant things"_. The presenter's own starter chip **"Is any screen showing right now?"** was answered correctly and then followed by an eight-step walkthrough of **Show the keys you press (Keyboard Screencast)**. Three independent defects. `owa_help_search` matched a term of four letters or more against the START of any longer word with no bound on the rest, so **"screen" matched "Screencast"** in a page TITLE, worth 14 -- 45 such pairs are live in this corpus (`copy`->`copyright`, `song`->`SongSelect`, `back`->`background`, `down`->`download`, `stop`->`stopwatch`), 229 occurrences, 10 of them in a title; the tail is now capped at three characters, and coverage is weighted by inverse frequency rather than counting words, and no longer squared. The two walkthrough buttons under an answer carried the top manual hit of the model's FIRST search and could never be revised -- not by a refined query, not by the page it actually opened with `owa_help_page`, not by it answering from `owa_list_screens`; a page the model OPENED now wins, a later search overwrites an earlier one, and a model that put its own card up is offered no button. And four supported questions named a recipe, `W-01b`, that no manual page carried: `build-manual.mjs` matched `W-\d+` only, so that heading was silently folded into W-01's page and never written, which `questions.test.mjs` could not catch because it checked the recipe id's SHAPE and called that "a real manual id". Graded on the 236-question corpus as a labelled retrieval set -- which nothing had ever done -- top-1 136 -> **140**, top-3 179 -> **186**, and 45 held-out volunteer paraphrases 21 -> **23**; per query, 9 fixed and 3 broken. **41% of supported questions still miss their own recipe**, filed as `EC-50` -- the reason rung 2 is still not marked reached. Total 786 -> **788**.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-01h** (**new CB-23: an answer on its way can be stopped.** Reported from the app with a screenshot of `Looking it up…` and no way back -- the box disabled, **Ask** disabled, and a question asked by mistake could only be waited out or the window closed. A question is up to ten model rounds and is paid for the whole way. **Ask** becomes **Stop** while an answer is coming, **Esc** does the same, and both ABORT the request rather than ignore its result -- verified live 2026-09-01 with ChatGPT, `net::ERR_ABORTED` on the round in flight, no late answer, and the happy path unchanged. A stop is never described as a service failure and never falls through to the offline bot. The row count also picks up CB-22, which was added without it. Total 784 -> **786**.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-01g** (**CB-06, CB-08 and CB-12 rewritten, plus a new CB-22: a THIRD provider, Kimi (Moonshot), answers in the chatbot, and every AI key row now says what its key is for.** Kimi speaks OpenAI's protocol, so the OpenAI tool loop was parameterised rather than cloned -- one `askOpenAiCompatible` plus a per-provider descriptor -- and the provider set is now declared once in `LLM_PROVIDER_MAP` with `Record<LlmProviderType, ...>` dispatch, replacing three two-way ternaries that each sent an unrecognised provider to OpenAI in silence. Kimi's budget rule is its own: every model offered thinks, so all get the 6000-token budget, and `reasoning_effort` goes only to `kimi-k3`. Verified live 2026-09-01 on a real key: `kimi-k3` and `kimi-k2.6` both answered in steps, **More models...** pulled `kimi-k2.7-code` off the account, and a bad model id fell back to the manual reading _"Kimi could not answer"_. Also fixed a latent data-loss bug in `setAISetting`, which rebuilt the secret blob from a hand-listed pair of fields: a Kimi-only key was deleted by the very save that stored it. Total 784 -> **785**.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-01f** (**CB-02 and CB-08 rewritten: both head-row switches are drop-downs.** They were segmented pairs of buttons beside a model picker that was already a `<select>`; six uppercase words spent most of a 460px window on two either/or choices and pushed the model name into an ellipsis. Both are `<select>`s now, the disabled provider says **needs an API key** in the option itself rather than on a hover nobody sees, and the provider picker's `aria-label` moved to **Which assistant answers** because it used to share the model picker's. `extra-work/verify-chatbot-e2e.mjs` drives them by option, not by click.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-01e** (**new CB-21: every answer now ends with buttons instead of a blank box.** Reported from the app with a screenshot: the assistant asked _"Would you like help turning one on for the congregation?"_ and the only way to say yes was to type it -- for a volunteer minutes before a service, often not a native English speaker, that is where the conversation stops. Answers only ever carried buttons when the model happened to read a manual page; anything answered from live app state carried nothing but **Copy**. The model now ends every answer with two or three short replies in the user's own voice, on an `OPTIONS:` line the code parses off and NEVER shows -- and when it writes none, the window reads the question the answer just asked (yes/no, or "A or B?"), and failing that offers the nearest questions from the 208-question corpus. Verified live 2026-09-01 with GPT-5 and on the offline guide. Cost: **0 extra tools, 0 extra tool-schema tokens**; the system prompt grew 263 chars (~66 tokens/round, +0.7%) after paying for it by compressing four redundant clauses. Total 783 -> **784**.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-01d** (**new CB-20: a walkthrough step that cannot be done for you goes and asks the assistant.** Reported from the app with a screenshot -- the card on W-06 step 3 (“The verse renders in the preview panel. **Double-click** it to present.”) read _“I could not do that one for you (nothing on screen to act on) - do it yourself, then press Skip.”_ Honest, and the end of the road for someone who pressed **Do it** precisely because they did not know what to do. Measured over the whole corpus: **68 of the manual's 251 steps (27%)** can reach that message -- a double-click, a drag, something only to be watched, a control not on screen yet. The card now relays the stuck step to the chat window, which looks at the live app and writes ONE line back onto the card; the chat window stays minimised, because restoring it would cover the control being pointed at. Verified live 2026-09-01 on the presenter, W-06 step 3: the measured failure rate is **1 in 12 (8%)**, median 12s, tracked by `rescue-failure-rate.mjs` rather than eyeballed, and one of them noticed the popup had been closed and said which button reopens it. With no chat window open it answers `unavailable` at once and the plain instruction comes back. Total 782 -> **783**.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-01c** (**new CB-19: an answer to the answer before it.** Reported from the app with a screenshot: the assistant said "No screen is currently showing. Would you like help to show a screen?", the user said **yes**, and the reply was "It sounds like you might need help or have a question. What can I assist you with?" Every question was being asked on its own -- `askLlmBot` took one question and built a fresh `messages` array, so nothing the assistant had just said was in front of it. The tab's recent turns now go back with each question, bounded (6 turns, 800 characters each, 2400 total, oldest dropped first) because the history is re-sent on every round of the tool loop. The offline bot, which cannot remember an offer either, no longer SEARCHES a bare "yes" -- the manual's best match for that word is the page about resetting the app's panels. Total 781 -> **782**.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-01b** (**new CB-18: the app's own labels reach the user in the language their app is actually in.** Reported from the app with a screenshot of a real answer — `Press F9 (Clear Bible - LUP-PREAH-KUMPI)` under a `# W-06 -` heading printed twice. The manual named every control in BOTH languages by hand; `toEnglishOnly` stripped that from search excerpts and was never applied to the whole-page read the model actually answers from, so both halves went through and were pasted. Six of those hand-written Khmer labels had also gone stale against the app, which nothing could catch. Documents now name a control as `[en:tran:Clear Bible]` and `tools/owa-devtools-mcp/tran.mjs` fills it in with what that key reads as in the language the app is DISPLAYING, on the way out of `owa_help_search`, `owa_help_page` and any guide card built from a recipe — so a guide's `find` matches the DOM in a Khmer window instead of missing every control. New `owa_tran` tool translates a label the model wrote itself; `owa_find_ui`/`owa_click`/`owa_type` retry with the translation on a miss; `owa_help_page` stops repeating the page title, drops cited recipe ids and the manual's screenshot marks. Total 780 -> **781**.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-09-01a** (**new CB-17: the ask box suggests questions as you type, and the Try asking chips stop being hardcoded.** Reported from the app with a screenshot circling the four chips: they were a fixed array of four strings per focus, and a user who wanted none of them had an empty box and no way to learn what the app can be asked. `tools/owa-devtools-mcp/questions/*.json` now carries **208 questions across 5 pages and 30 sections** — one file per page of the app, sectioned by the panel the user is looking at — each pinned to the live-verified `W-xx` recipe, control, menu path, keystroke and tools that answer it, so a picked question is a lookup rather than a search round. The window ranks them locally from bundled JSON, so suggestions work with no key, no network and no tool call per keystroke; the chips are the corpus's own `starterRank` order; and the new `owa_list_questions` tool hands the model the same list, so a question too vague to search for is answered with what the app CAN do instead of a guess. Total 779 -> **780**.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-08-31m** (**CB-09 and CB-11 extended: the help window gets out of the walkthrough’s way.** Reported from the app with a screenshot: the chatbot popup sat over the middle of the presenter while a card told the user to press a control behind it — measured live, 515×540 of a 1243×837 client area, **12% of the presenter’s named controls** hidden, and the guide has no way to see an OS window. Starting a walkthrough now minimises the chatbot window and closing the card brings it back; a window that is not over the guided window is left alone, and one the user minimised (or brought back mid-walkthrough) is never touched. Total 779.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-08-31l** (**CB-11 and CB-09 extended: the ring lands on the control the step means when the panel is already OPEN.** The `j` entry below fixed this same symptom and was verified _from a collapsed layout_ only -- which is why it came back. An OPEN panel drew its name nowhere at all, so “Open the Background panel” had nothing to match but the `Background:` transition button beside the screen preview, and that is what was ringed. Three fixes: every resizable pane now carries its ENGLISH name as `data-widget-name` whether open or collapsed; the matcher reads the PARENT PATH, so a step can say `Background > Videos` and a match reports the panel it sits in; and a recipe that qualifies a bold with a container noun (“the **Background** panel”) scopes the rest of the step to it. Two more from the same screenshot: the card now moves itself out from under the ring, and the ring animates its COLOUR, not only its size. Total 779.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-08-31k** (**CB-11 extended: a step whose control is inside a right-click menu is now DONE, in two presses.** Reported from the app with a screenshot — W-21 step 2, “Right-click an empty part of the list … and choose **Download From URL**”, answered “I could not do that one for you (nothing on screen to act on)”. The guide had no right-click at all, the step's target is a region with no words on it, and the step is two actions. All three fixed. Two documentation defects came out of the same screenshot: the recipe named a **+** button that does not exist (it is the **⋮ More Options** button), and a label with a Khmer twin left a husk — “Download From URL (URL)”. Total 779.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-08-31j** (**CB-11 and CB-09 extended: a walkthrough step rings the control it MEANS.** Reported from the app with a screenshot: “Open the Background panel” ringed the `Background:` transition button beside the screen preview, and pressing **Do it** opened the transition menu instead of the panel. Two causes, both fixed: the collapsed panel bar was a clickable `div` that never said it was a button, and the matcher broke ties on label LENGTH rather than on which element is actually named those words. Found while verifying: the same recipe's step 2 rang **Bible Lookup**, because “Ok” matched inside “lo-ok-up” — in demo mode that would have PRESSED it. Total 779.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-08-31i** (**CB-11 rewritten: the walkthrough presses keyboard shortcuts.** A step that named no control ("Close the dialog with the red ✕ button or **Ctrl+Q**", "Press **F9** to take the verse off screen") used to answer "I could not do that one for you (nothing on screen to act on)" — on 82 of the manual's 251 steps, a third of every walkthrough. A step that names a shortcut now presses it, and the user pressing it themselves advances the card. 82 → 69 un-actionable steps; W-06 goes from 2 of 6 steps actionable to 5 of 6. Total 779.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-08-31h** (**new CB-16: a symptom question is diagnosed from the live app, not guessed at.** "Nothing is showing on the projector" and the same thing said as a non-native speaker used to be answered with generic hardware advice (the projector's power, its cable) or with a GUESSED control name; both now open by calling `owa_list_screens` and name the real show/hide control. CB-03 amended: what one `owa_help_page` returns to the model is now capped per corpus. Total 778 -> **779**.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-08-31g** (new CB-15: every chat tab has a menu, and a chat can be locked.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-08-31f** (**new NAV-22 and NAV-23: the popup windows
remember where you put them.** Every `window.open` popup -- chatbot, Settings, the
document/lyric/bible-note/web editors, the reader popup -- now reopens at the position
and size the user last left it, kept per html page in `popupWinBoundsMap` in
`setting.json`, and **Window -> Reset Position and Size** now forgets that map and puts
the open popups back over their opener as well as the main window. Total 775 -> **777**.)

Previous: **2026-08-31e** (**new CB-12, CB-13, CB-14; CB-06 and CB-08
rewritten: the help window holds several chats, and the setting block belongs to the
tab.** A persisted tab strip (`local-storage/chatbot-sessions`, capped at 12 tabs × 60
messages) carries the conversation, the draft, the focus, the provider AND the model;
the model is a picker with speed and price on the hover plus a **More models…** that
lists the account's own catalogue; every answer has **Copy** and every question **Ask
again**; and a window with no key offers **Open AI settings**. Total 772 → **775**.)

Previous: **2026-08-31d** (**new CB-11: the walkthrough can drive the app.**
**Do it for me** starts the same guide in demo mode — **Do it** clicks the ringed
control or types into it, one step per press, **Skip** hands a step back to the user,
and a step with nothing on screen says so instead of pretending. Total 771 → **772**.)

Previous: **2026-08-31c** (**new CB-09 and CB-10, CB-03 rewritten: the chatbot
walks the user through the app, and answers for the window they are in.** Any manual
answer can now be turned into a numbered card drawn inside the app window with the
control for each step ringed in red (`owa_guide_start` / `_step` / `_status`), which
skips steps the user has already done. Answers are scoped by `surface` — recorded per
manual page at build time — so the reader is never handed the presenter's Ctrl+B; the
offline bot searches the manual only; and nothing user-facing carries a document id, a
file path or the Khmer twin of a label. Total 769 → **771**.)

Previous: **2026-08-31b** (**CB-01 and CB-06 rewritten, new CB-08: the chatbot has a
toolbar button and a provider switch.** A **🤖** button now opens it from the top-right of
both the presenter and the reader, left of **?**, and disappears with the AI master switch.
Inside, **Claude** / **ChatGPT** pick who answers: a provider with no key is disabled, the
model name is shown, the choice is remembered in `local-storage/chatbot-llm-provider`, and a
failed call names the provider it fell back from. Total 768 → **769**.)

Previous: **2026-08-31a** (**new CB-01..CB-07: the built-in help chatbot** — `Help → App Help (Chatbot)` opens `chatbot.html`, which talks to the app's own MCP server (`owa-devtools-mcp`, served over HTTP by the main process) and answers from the manual bundled into the build, from live app state, and by outlining the control it is describing in the real window. A Presenter/Reader switch decides which half of the app an answer is about; an AI key in Settings → Others upgrades it from lookup to conversation; **Enable AI features** off means the next launch opens neither the chatbot nor the CDP endpoint. **The CDP port is no longer 9223 or any fixed number** — every instance publishes its own to `<temp>/open-worship-app-cdp/<pid>.json`. Total 761 → **768**.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-08-30m** (**new PR-30/PR-31 + CM-98/CM-99: a whole Bible Note FILE now exports and imports.** Only a single note ITEM could leave the app (`.owabn`, from an item's ⋮); the file itself — all its items, the background attached to it, and every image/audio/video its items embed — now exports as `.owanote.tar.gz` from the note file's ⋮ and imports from the **Bible Notes** panel header (Import / Import From URL / drop). It rides the shared single-item bundle rather than a fifth hand-rolled archive: two new archive kinds (`note`, `note-asset`) plus three optional hooks on `singleItemArchiveHelpers` for files an item's own CONTENTS point at. **CM-69 corrected**: the item-level `Import` was gated on the file being named `Default`, so an exported item could only ever come back into that one file — the gate is gone. Verified live 2026-08-30 on the reader: a note holding a 28 MB video, an mp3 and two images exported (manifest tagging 1 `note` + 4 `note-asset`), imported by drop as `name (1).own` beside the original with every path re-pointed at the temp-files copies, and the same round trip again through a password-protected `.owanote.enc`. PR 29 → **31**, CM 97 → **99**, total 757 → **761**.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-08-30l** (**RD-111/RD-112: a mark row now GOES somewhere, a verse row can be dragged to another note file, and both wear the bible's own face.** Clicking a mark opens its verse as another bible view instead of only flashing it, and editing a comment moved to the mark's `⋮`. The verse row carries a second drag payload so it can be dropped on another note file — which surfaced that `deserializeDragData` never had a `NOTE_ITEM` branch, making EVERY note-item drop in the app dead code since the feature was written; fixed and covered by a new `src/helper/dragHelpers.test.ts`. `useBibleFontFamily` now paints the whole verse block, the comment editor (title + textarea) and the hover tooltip, so a Khmer mark is not read in the UI font beside a passage in Battambang. The hover tooltip's grace went 250ms → 1200ms: the timer restarts on every mousemove outside the marked words, so it was stopping to READ that spent it, and the tooltip vanished before it could be used. Verified live 2026-08-30. Counts unchanged.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-08-30k** (**RD-111 redrawn: the marks list wears its own marks.** A highlight under a verse row was a grey swatch dot beside the words in italic quotes; the words now carry the wash itself and a commented phrase carries the wavy underline, from the same custom properties the reader paints with — the panel is a miniature of the page and needs no key. The row is led by `bi-highlighter`, its chevron rotates, its count is a quiet chip that steps aside while the row is open, and Bootstrap's filled `text-bg-secondary` badge is gone. A hairline stem under the verse was tried and removed: the cue sheet's index gutter already brackets the group. Verified live 2026-08-30, dark and light. Counts unchanged.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-08-30j** (**RD-111 grows a row's actions, and Move To works at last.** A verse row now carries a `⋮` of its own and so does every mark under it; the row's menu offers **Add to Bible List**, **Move To** and **Delete**, and the row can be DRAGGED onto the Bible list, where it arrives as an ordinary bible item. New **RD-112**. `moveNoteItemTo` was passing the folder name `bible-notes` where `DirSource.getInstance` wants the SETTING name `select-dir-bible-notes`, so **every** “Move To” and “Move All Items To” in Bible Notes has been answering `No other notes found` however many note files existed — fixed, and now the only reason the verse row's own Move To works. Both floating surfaces (the selection toolbar and the comment hover tool) portal to `document.body`, OUTSIDE the element carrying `data-bs-theme`, so every `--bs-*` token resolved light and the toolbar came up white on the dark reader; both carry the theme themselves now. Verified live 2026-08-30. RD 111 → **112**, total 756 → **757**.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-08-30i** (**new RD-108..111: verse text can be highlighted and commented.** Selecting words in a bible view now raises a floating toolbar — six highlighter colours, Add Comment, Remove Marks — and the marks are stored in the user's own `.note` files as a second kind of item, one VERSE item per verse, so they appear in the Bible Notes panel beside the bible notes and ride the existing save, watch, archive and backup machinery. Marks are painted with the CSS Custom Highlight API, which adds no DOM per mark and is the only way to paint over the `dangerouslySetInnerHTML` words-of-Christ markup. Commented words hover to a tooltip that edits or deletes. Verified live 2026-08-30 on the reader, Genesis 22:1: highlight + comment created, painted on exactly the selected characters, listed under `Default` as a collapsible verse row, re-anchored after a full reload, recoloured, and deleted — the verse row disappearing with its last mark. RD 107 → **111**, total 752 → **756**.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-08-30h** (**RD-94: a directed edge now SHOWS its direction, and every edge meets the box border.** `child` between two boxes did not say which of them was the child: the arrowhead rode an SVG `marker-end`, which sits at the path's end — the target box's CENTRE, underneath the box — and was painted black by a CSS rule that matched nothing, so it was invisible twice over. Edges are now clipped to the two boxes they join and a parentage edge carries a filled arrowhead in the line's own colour, just outside the box it points at. Verified live 2026-08-30 on the reader, Abraham + 8 children: 8 arrows, none under a box, holding through collapse and a node drag.)

Previous: **matrixVersion: 2026-08-30g** (**five ID-colliding rows renumbered; counts re-derived.** The July canvas-empty-area menu rows and the audio-scrubber seek row had been sharing IDs with newer archive-era / mini-screen rows: canvas CM-36..39 → **CM-94..97**, audio-seek SP-18 → **SP-22** (the archive Export/Import CM-36..39 and the mini-screen ⋮ SP-18 keep their IDs — user-workflows `Verify:` lines cite them). Footer counts re-derived from the table: GL 23→24, RD 106→107, total 750 → **752**; the presenting-flow deep-mode denominator line now says `69 − EXCLUDED`, matching its own scope set.)

Previous: **2026-08-30f** (**new RD-107: the auto-scroll button says what its four gestures do.** Click / double-click / right-click / Alt+right-click on the `bi-chevron-double-down` were stated only in a tooltip that the running state then overwrites with a bare number - and not at all on a touch screen or on the screen preview. A horizontal `⋯` now appears beside it while it is running and opens a menu naming all four, each entry firing the real gesture. Verified live 2026-08-30 in both the presenter's bible previewer and the screen previewer's copy: `0.07 → 0.14 → 0.35 → 0.28 → stopped`.)

Previous: **2026-08-30e** (**GL-24: one resting alpha for every ⋮, 0.1.** The shared button and every older one that predates it now rest at the value the colour note already used, and all go to full on hover/focus. Re-measured against the 22px `app-cue-list` rows from 30d: 22×22px box, one 2px inset, `dy 0` on every leaf row, header line on every container. Verified live 2026-08-30 — 44 ⋮ on the reader, 41 shared + 3 legacy, every one computing 0.1.)

Previous: **2026-08-30d** (**PR-15/PR-22/PR-23: the Bibles and Bible Notes panels are drawn as one cue sheet.** Both panel roots now carry `app-cue-list` (`src/bible-list/cueList.scss`), which drops the Bootstrap row capsule for a flush row and a 2px left RAIL carrying rank by colour — `--app-line` at rest, `--app-accent` on the open folder, `--app-on-air` on a row live to a screen — and turns the floated `attr(data-index)` number into a real in-flow gutter with a hairline rule. Notes folders use `bi-journals`/`bi-journal-text` instead of the Bibles panel's book, and the add-item `+` is an `app-ghost-button`, not inline green. Rows measure 22px (was 27-28) and the header 26px (was 30), so ~25% more rows fit; the path row no longer overflows its own panel. Verified live 2026-08-30 on the reader in both themes.)

Previous: **2026-08-30c** (**GL-24: the `⋮` now has ONE geometry — the row action rail.** Placed per-host, the same button came out 18×24px in one list and 18×17px in the next, at three different right insets, and on a file row it hung off `top: 0` of a row 294px tall — level with one of its own children's ⋮. It now carries its own `22×22px` box and sits in a rail pinned to the row's border edge, capped at one row-line so it centres on a leaf row and stays on the header line of a container. Row-hover arming was tried and dropped: it reached only some hosts and does nothing on the touch screens this button exists for. Verified live 2026-08-30 — 14 list rows across three families measured one inset, one box, `dy ≤ 2px`.)

Previous: **2026-08-30b** (**new GL-24 — the `⋮` context-menu button is now app-wide.** Every surface whose actions lived ONLY behind a right-click carries one: file rows, bible items and notes, lookup records and history, bible views, slide previews, background cards, presenting flow rows and preview frames, screen previews, graph boxes, Resources rows, cross-reference cards, foreground buttons, the verse audio player and canvas item cards. RD-92's lookup-row `⋮` is one instance of it. Deliberately NOT rowed: inline name/verse links in prose, the 20×15px colour swatch, the 5px splitter (GL-17 — its menu is also on View → Reset Widgets Size and the quick-resize arrows) and the slide-editor canvas boxes (their menu is on the Canvas Items tool list, which does carry one). Verified live 2026-08-30 on the reader.)

Previous: **2026-08-30a** (**RD-57 and RD-92: a record's kind icon moved from the detail panel's title bar into its body, and every lookup row grew a ⋮.** The icon now leads the summary it describes instead of competing with the title bar's own buttons, and the **Open Graph Preview** menu that was right-click-only on a lookup row is now also on a visible **⋮ More Options** button at the row's right end. Verified live 2026-08-30 on the reader, km records: 24 rows carried the ⋮, the menu opened from it, and the three panel kinds showed `bi-person-fill` / `bi-geo-alt-fill` / `bi-book-half` in the body with an empty title bar.)

Previous: **2026-08-29n** (**new RD-91 — a translated record carries its
ENGLISH name beside its own, and search accepts either language.** `bible-note` 0.4.0-dev
parses a record's `kjvName` and folds it into the same `searchText` as `name`/`oldName`
(ranked by `min(rank(name), rank(kjvName))`), so `Moses` finds ម៉ូសេ with the `km` dataset
loaded and `ម៉ូសេ` still does. The app renders it the way a Bible book reads
`លោកុប្បត្តិ (Genesis)` on the three surfaces it owns — the lookup rows (RD-54), the
"in your reading" rows (RD-72) and a detail widget's TITLE (RD-57) — through ONE helper,
`getRecordKjvName`, reimplemented in `lookupPresentationHelpers` rather than imported from
`bible-note` so the pre-load surfaces still pull none of that ~46MB graph. The reading list
gets it from a new `kjvNames` array in the labels sidecar, captured from the English label
the pass had already written rather than from the translated package's own field, so a record
the translation misses is covered too; `LOOKUP_TEXT_INDEX_VERSION` is bumped to 3 and
`checkIsRecordLabelsValid` now length-checks it. Nothing is added under `en` — the name
already IS the English one, and `Moses (Moses)` must never appear. Verified live 2026-08-29,
km and en, names and locations, list + reading panel + detail title.)

Previous: **2026-08-29m** (**RD-10, RD-49, RD-50, RD-51, RD-52: the Cross Reference
panel is restyled and two of its marks now say what they mean.** It rendered a bordered,
rounded box per verse inside a dotted `--bs-info-text-emphasis` card, with the theme, the
reference pill and the card title all painted the same cyan — three ranks of information at
one weight, so the sentence explaining WHY a group of verses is together read as a caption.
It is now a citation block: the theme flush left in `--app-ink`, its verses hanging off a
single 2px `--app-line` rail, hover lighting that rail's segment in `--app-accent`. Themes
set in `columns: 19rem` in place of a `flex-wrap` that aligned each row to its tallest theme
and left a dead half-screen under the short one. New `app-xref-meta` line: `N Themes ·
M Verses`. The card lightbulb split into `bi-cpu` (AI wrote this) and `bi-translate` (this
heading was machine-translated) — the latter now shown only when `titleEn` really differs,
where it used to sit on every heading including on an English bible. `breakItem` cuts at a
word and appends `…` only when it truncated (`toPreviewText`); rows take the keyboard; the
section toggle is a real `button` with `aria-expanded`; the row tooltip and the empty state
were both rewritten to describe what actually happens. New file
`src/bible-cross-refs/BibleCrossRefComp.scss`; five new `km` keys. Verified live 2026-08-29,
dark and light, KJV and ពគប. RD-10, RD-49, RD-50, RD-51, RD-52 rewritten.)
Previous: **2026-08-29l** (**RD-09, RD-45, RD-46: the Find results list is restyled,
and its chunk footer no longer draws a button per page.** A common word pages into the
thousands, and the footer drew EVERY number — 1556 buttons in a 10,260px-tall grid, laid out
and painted on every result render to fill a 100px strip. It now draws a window
(`toFindPageWindow`): first, last, the neighbours of the chunk being read, and the loaded
chunks, ~12 buttons whatever the find returns, led by `N verses found`. The matched text gets
its own class `app-found-match` in `--app-accent` with an underline — it shared
`app-found-highlight` with the reference pill beside it and the chunk marker above it, so the
one thing explaining why a row is on screen painted the same blue as the furniture. Result
cards became rows, the reference became a marker, and the book filter is quiet on **All

shared `app-ghost-button`. Verified live 2026-08-29, dark and light. RD-09, RD-45, RD-46
rewritten.)

`safeStorage` — the AI

designed behaviour, not a regression. Where the OS has no credential store the app refuses

**Import From SongSelect** — a floating search popup that downloads

Previous: **2026-08-22** (**the KJV row in Settings → Bible grew a **Reset Bible XML** button — new ST-51.** The `bi-arrow-counterclockwise` `btn-warning` appears only on the row whose key is `KJV`; it confirms, then overwrites that row’s own XML file with the app-embedded KJV (the same data the empty-state "Create KJV Bible XML" of ST-28 writes), clears the sibling `.xml.cache` folder and the in-memory parse, and force-reloads every window. Verified live 2026-08-22 in the Setting window: button present on KJV and on no other of the 20 installed bibles, confirm copy correct, file rewritten, cache folder emptied and re-derived, reader re-read Genesis 18 from the replaced file. **ST-28 rewritten in the same change:** "Create KJV Bible XML" is no longer empty-state-only — it is also the FIRST ROW of the `Bibles XML` list whenever that list has no `KJV` key, so a trashed KJV can be rebuilt without emptying the whole list first. Verified live: with KJV trashed the list led with a **+ Create KJV Bible XML** row, the click rebuilt the translation (`King James Version`, ↺ present) and the row went away. Denominator 719 → **720**.)
Previous: **2026-08-12** (**the advance-lookup panel's tab strip became a `select` —
RD-41 and RD-72 rewritten.** **Find** / **Cross Reference** / **Location-Name (KJV)** needed
409px of tabs in a 366px header, so the third label was permanently cut off ("Location-Name
(K…") and only reachable by scrolling the strip sideways. The header now holds ONE
full-width `select.form-select-sm`, with the active view's icon (`bi-search` /
`bi-signpost-split` / `bi-geo-alt-fill`) rendered beside it — an `option` can only carry
text, so the icon cannot ride inside the list. The body swap and the `bible-search-tab`
setting are unchanged, as is `viewController.openBibleSearch` (the `Open in Cross Reference`
context-menu entry, CM-74/RD-14, still selects the view). Verified live 2026-08-12 on the
reader: picked all three, icon + title follow the choice, bodies load. No row added or
removed; denominator unchanged at **719**.)
Previous: **2026-08-11** (**the file watch moved to the DATA DIR, and a lyric's slides
now refresh on a content change — new XW-08, XW-09, XW-10, PM-127.** Every list used to watch
its own directory (`useDirSourceWatching`, one `fs.watch` per mounted `DirSource`); there is now
ONE **recursive** watch on the **data parent dir**, started lazily by `watchDataDir()` the first
time anything registers a `FileSource` listener (`src/helper/dirWatchingHelpers.ts`,
`FileSource.registerEventListener*`). So an edit propagates even to a surface whose list is not
mounted, and a write into a **sidecar** (`<file>.histories`, `-htmls`, `-images`) fires an
`update` for the file it belongs to. **XW-08** is the bug the change fixes: a lyric edited
elsewhere left the **Stage Previewer** panes showing the pre-edit slides — the panes re-rendered
but re-derived from `LyricAppDocumentStageAbstract`'s 3-minute `CacheManager`, and
`getOpenLyricPreviewer()` handed back the cached `openLyric` without re-reading `content`.
`LyricSlidesPreviewerComp` now clears every stage's cache on `update`, so ALL stages refresh, not
just the one that happened to miss the cache. **XW-09** pins the watch itself (data-dir-wide,
lazy, sidecar mapping) and **XW-10** the readdir half — a file added or removed on disk must
reach the list. ⚠️ **XW-10 was measured FAILING 2026-08-11**; both of its causes were fixed in
code and the row is now **re-verified live and PASSING (2026-08-13, run `20260813-2138`)** —
both halves, add and delete, refreshed the Documents list unaided in under 3 s.
**PM-127** is the Stage Previewer header's new **⋮ → Reload**. Denominator 715 → **719**.
Two later corrections, neither re-verified live: watch events are now **debounced 500 ms**
before any filesystem work (a media download writes its file in hundreds of chunks and each one
was costing a stat and a readdir), so allow ~1 s in XW-08/XW-09/XW-10 rather than expecting the
refresh in the same instant; and a failed media download now names its cause in the toast —
see MD-01.)
Previous: **2026-08-10e** (**the media binaries left the app — new MD-05, MD-06.**
`yt-dlp`/`ffmpeg`/`qjs` are no longer bundled (~36 MB out of every installer): they ship as a
separate `bin-<ver>.tar.gz` the user installs from **Settings → Others → Extra Binaries** into
`<data parent>/extra-bin/`. So the MD block gains a **precondition** — on a machine where the
pack is absent, MD-01/MD-02 fail for a reason that is not a bug. **MD-05** covers the panel
(install, version readout, the `Update to <ver>` button, and `Re-extract`, which must work with
no network because the `bin-*.tar.gz` is deliberately kept on disk); **MD-06** covers the guard
that turns a missing pack into a **Media Tools Required** dialog jumping to that panel instead of
a spawn failure — and asserts the cancel path does not double-toast. In dev the download is
mocked from `extra-work/experiment-building/release/`, which `npm i` builds. `extra-bin` is NOT
teardown: leave it installed. Every `bin-helper/…` path in the MD notes is rewritten to
`extra-bin/…`. Denominator 713 → **715**.)

cleans up its own `temp-xml` download (ST-41), the **Key is missing** dialog offers every root
attribute split on `[.,\s]` minus the taken keys — which is exactly how a `link="…/GEN.23.ពគប"`
hands you `ពគប` as a one-click button (ST-42) — and a raw import then leaves the bible on
`locale="en-US"` with ASCII digits and English book names (ST-44). The Info editor's
`🌎 Choose Locale` / `#️⃣ Edit Numbers Map` / `📚 Edit Books Map` fix all three, **in that order**,
because the last two read the locale sitting in the editor (ST-45..ST-48); the save then re-groups
the bible under its locale in the reader's key menu (ST-49). ST-50 records that **Move to Trash
leaves `<key>.xml.cache` behind** — scratch-bible runs must sweep it. Denominator 703 → **713**,
and the stale row-count block is re-derived with it (it still claimed 696/ST 33).)
Previous: **2026-08-10c** (**the mismatched-password re-ask no longer throws away what
was typed and picked — AP-04, ST-37 rewritten.** Export Data and Export Bible Data used to
answer a mistyped confirmation with a `showAppAlert` and then re-open themselves. The alert
unmounts the popup, so the re-ask came back with the password fields BLANK and the item
selection reset to everything — one more Ok exported the whole list UNPROTECTED. Both now run
the same `MAX_PASSWORD_ATTEMPTS` loop the other three kinds already used, re-rendering the
same popup with the complaint as `invalidMessage`, so `ArchivePasswordComp` and
`ArchiveItemSelectorComp` keep their state. Also: the two fields now simply have to AGREE
(both empty settles the complaint; a confirmation typed against an empty password is flagged
instead of silently exporting unprotected), and **ST-40**'s `M skipped` counts everything the
bundle held that did not come in. Plus four drift corrections found live — **CM-36** (Export
sits after Print, not last), **CM-37** (`Open Shared Link` is a top-level list entry, not an
`Add Items` child), **NAV-17** (the row is labelled `Bible Notes`), **ST-02/ST-09** (Settings
has three tabs: General · Bible · Others). No row added or removed; denominator unchanged at
**703**.)
Previous: **2026-08-10b** (**new ST-34..ST-40 — Export / Import Bible Data.** The XML
bibles of Settings → Bible now have their own bundle, `.owabdata.tar.gz` (`.owabdata.enc`
when protected), picked through the same one-dialog selector + password the data archive
uses and dropped anywhere onto the Bible XML section to import. The rule the rows exist to
pin down: an import **never overwrites and never duplicates** — a key that already exists
**case-insensitively** (`kjv` against an installed `KJV`), a key colliding with an earlier
entry of the same bundle, and a file whose key cannot be read (or disagrees with the
manifest, which is untrusted) each become a RED, un-tickable row and are skipped, while the
clean rows still import. Denominator 696 → **703**.)

Earlier: **2026-08-08i** (**new RD-53..RD-64 — the Names & locations lookup**, a
floating widget opened from the `bi-person-fill`+`bi-geo-alt-fill` button in the bible
lookup input group (reader, the presenter's Bible Lookup popup, and the Bible Note
window). It browses the biblical names/locations dataset, opens a record as its own
floating panel, chases references between panels, resolves verse references to KJV text,
and embeds a Google map for a location that has coordinates.
**RD-61 and RD-63 are the load-bearing ones.** RD-61: the map frame MUST keep
`sandbox="allow-scripts"` — every window runs `webSecurity:false` + `nodeIntegration:true`

- `contextIsolation:false`, so an unsandboxed cross-origin frame can reach
  `window.top.require`. RD-63: the ~34MB dataset is reference-counted and shared between the
  lookup panel and the detail widgets — one fetch while anything is open, released when the
  last one closes. Both measured live.
  Note `will-navigate`/`will-redirect` in the main process now ignore SUB-frame navigations
  (`electron/ElectronMainController.ts`); without that the map embed's own redirect was
  cancelled and popped open in the system browser.
  **Denominator re-derived from the table: 658 → 678** — the hand-written count below had
  drifted 15 rows behind again (651 vs an actual 666) before these 12 were added.)
  Previous: **2026-08-08h** (**a website canvas item and a web BACKGROUND are now
  SCREENSHOTS, not live iframes**. An `<iframe>` keeps its page's scripts, timers and media
  running for as long as it is in the DOM, so a document full of website slides used to run
  every one of them at once. They now render a cached screenshot on the editor canvas, the
  Canvas Items list, slide thumbnails, print and the presenter's MINI screen, and become a
  live page only on the PROJECTED screen. The slide editor and the mini screen additionally
  swap in a live iframe after a **1-second hover** (a web background does not — the whole
  `#background` container is `pointer-events: none`). New **ED-45**, **ED-46**, **PM-124**,
  **PM-125**; **ED-41** and **CM-43** rewritten. Denominator 654 → **658**.)
  Previous: **2026-08-08g** (**the Finder popup window is gone — Find is now a bar PINNED
  INSIDE the window it searches**. `Ctrl/⌘+F` (or **Edit → Find**) attaches a
  `WebContentsView` to the top-right of the host window; `finder.html` is still a
  `list_pages` target but it is no longer a separate OS window, and a search no longer fans
  out to every other window. **PU-01**, **PU-07**, **KB-51**, **KB-52** and **KB-54**
  rewritten (KB-52 was `Ctrl+Q` closes the finder window, now `Shift+Enter` steps to the
  previous match). New **PU-19** and **PU-20**.
  **PU-19 is the load-bearing one**: `findInPage` walks EVERYTHING in a web contents —
  nested iframes and `input` values included — so a bar living in the page finds the query
  typed into itself. Measured live: an in-page bar returned `0/1` for a probe string that
  exists nowhere else; the pinned view returns `0/0`. Do not "simplify" the bar back into
  the page. Denominator 652 → **654**.)
  Previous: **2026-08-08f** (**ED-40..ED-44 — the Background panel can now be DRAGGED
  onto the editor canvas**: an image/video/audio/web/camera/color dragged out of the bottom
  Background panel becomes the matching canvas item at the drop point, instead of the drop
  being silently ignored. A color dropped ON a box recolors that box rather than covering it.
  Two latent bugs went with it: the insert position used to be measured against whatever the
  drop landed on (a box, not the canvas), and the accept dim was never cleared on a drop that
  landed on a box. Verified live 2026-08-08.
  Previous: **2026-08-08e** (**PL-102 — a `Slide: Media Control` now STOPS on unselect**:
  presenting another slide on the screen it ran on, or clearing that screen, pauses every media
  of the outgoing slide and disarms the controller. Before this the swap was simply REFUSED
  (**Media is Playing**) and a sheet that played a slide's audio could not move on. Media the
  operator started by hand is unchanged and still refuses. Verified live 2026-08-08.
  Previous: **2026-08-08d** (**PL-95 — a CC'd `Next: Timeout` may now be armed PER
  ATTACHMENT**: its CC row carries a `stopwatch` glyph (and **Change Timing** in its menu)
  that opens the element's own arming question and stores the answer on the attachment, so
  one timeout element can wait 4s under one line and 30s under another. Hollow glyph = still
  reading the element; filled = holding its own, with **Use Element Timing** to hand it back.
  Absence still means the element's, so re-arming the element still moves every follower that
  has none. Verified live 2026-08-08. Previous: **2026-08-08c** (**PL-61 — the presenting flow preview is no longer one-at-a-time**: several widgets may be open together, one per file, each with its own persisted rect (`floating-widget-rect-presenting-flow-preview-<sanitized path>`), its own run cursor, its own folding and its own auto-next clock; the thumbnail zoom stays shared. Verified live 2026-08-08. Previous: **2026-08-08b** (**new PL-102 — `Slide: Media Control`**, the first action
  that drives the media INSIDE a slide and the only one that is NOT offered by `Add Action`:
  it is authored from the slide it controls (**Add Media Control**, under **Add CC Elements**
  on any slide or document row), its settings live on the ATTACHMENT rather than on the
  element, and its screen pin NARROWS where the host landed instead of replacing it. Play /
  Pause / Stop, with a delay, a media start point, a stop point (after N seconds or at a media
  time), a volume and a speed; the speed now rides the slide-video sync so the projection runs
  at it too. Denominator 651 → **652**; presenting flow deep mode is now 69 rows
  (`PL-81..PL-102`). Earlier: **2026-08-08** — **the `Add Action` menu folds ALL of its clearing behind one
  `Clear Screen` (លុបអេក្រង់) row.** **PL-71** amended: the second menu is SEVEN rows, not
  twelve — `Clear Screen`, the two screen show/hide actions, the run actions — and the
  five broad clears plus the `Other Clear FG Items` row moved one level in, so the per-widget
  clears of **PL-74** are now four levels deep. No id changed, so sheets already written are
  untouched. Also new **PL-101** — a fifth run action, **`Next: Clear Interval`**
  (បន្ទាប់៖ បញ្ឈប់រៀងរាល់ចន្លោះពេល): the loop's off switch written as a LINE of the sheet, since an
  interval otherwise ends only by the pill, by the widget closing or with the run — all of them
  the operator being at the machine. It asks nothing when added, ends a running (or paused)
  interval and **only** an interval, and says nothing when there was none. Denominator 650 → **651**. Earlier:
  **2026-08-07i** (**Ctrl (⌘) + click on a Documents row is now the shortcut
  for `Open Slides Preview`.** It toggles that document's floating widget and never selects
  the document; on the row that IS selected it opens nothing but is still swallowed, so a
  modified click never falls back to plain selecting. **PM-118** and **PM-119** amended (no
  new rows, denominator stays **650**); the menu entry's tooltip is where the shortcut is
  advertised — deliberately not the `context-menu-shortcut-key` badge, which is positioned
  over the right end of the label and is unreadable under a label this long. Earlier:
  **2026-08-07h** (**a Documents row can now open its own floating slides
  previewer.** New **PM-118** (`Open Slides Preview` on every kind of Documents row — one
  widget per file, several at once, the same previewer tree the centre panel renders,
  Note pane and editing strip included), **PM-119** (the exclusivity rule: a document is
  previewed in exactly ONE place, so the entry is disabled on the selected row and
  selecting a document closes its widget) and **PM-120** (per-widget zoom under
  `presenter-item-thumbnail-size-<path>`, and the new `data-slides-previewer-scope`
  that makes arrow-key stepping and touch-drag autoscroll answer for the FOCUSED
  previewer instead of the first one in the document). Denominator 647 → **650**.
  Earlier: **2026-08-07g** (**every Stage Previewer chip now carries a gear that opens
  a per-stage slide-style panel.** New **PM-116** (the gear itself — on the base chip too,
  one floating widget that RETARGETS rather than a second one) and **PM-117** (the controls:
  padding / background opacity / extra font size / theme / a custom-CSS box, and Reset).
  The four knobs used to be hard-coded fields on `LyricAppDocument` that nothing ever
  assigned; they now live in one JSON blob per stage under the **unprefixed** setting
  `lyric-stage-style-<stage>`, so the presenter, the reader and the screen all read the same
  key. The custom CSS is **appended** to the stage's own layout css, never substituted for
  it — a custom rule on stage 0 that made its chords reappear would be the regression to
  watch. **SC-08** amended: its font-parity assertion should now be run AFTER changing
  `extraFontSize` in the panel, which is what would catch a divergent key. Denominator
  645 → **647**. Earlier: **2026-08-07f** (**the media block now cleans up after itself.** New
  **MD-04**: the video and the audio pulled by MD-01/MD-02 are deleted again — through the
  row's own **Move to Trash** (CM-06 coverage on a background media row, hidden while the
  item is on a screen), with an on-disk fallback for what never reached the list, and a
  sweep of earlier runs' leftovers BEFORE the download. The block writes ~100 MB per run and
  the app de-duplicates by suffixing rather than overwriting, so an uncleaned block grows
  the user's data dir every pass — 17 stale copies (≈635 MB) had
  accumulated by 2026-08-07 and were swept by hand. A `(1)` suffix on a run's FIRST download now reads as "MD-04
  was skipped last time". Row-count line also fixed: it had never included the **AP** block,
  so the denominator goes 636 → **645** (644 existing rows + MD-04). Earlier: **2026-08-07e**
  (**optional password protection on EVERY export.** Each of
  the five archive exports — data (**NAV-17**), document/lyric (**CM-36**), presenting flow
  (**PL-39**), bible list (**CM-38**) and bible note item — now opens a dialog with
  **Password** + **Confirm Password** before it writes anything. Empty means "no password"
  and produces byte-for-byte the bundle it always did; a password writes `<name>.<kind>.enc`
  (`.owapf.enc`, `.owadoc.enc`, `.owbible.enc`, `.owabn.enc`, `.owadata.enc`) instead of
  `.tar.gz`/`.tar`. Import detects a protected bundle by its container magic — NOT by the
  name, so a renamed one still works and a plain one is never prompted for — and asks, with
  three tries and a **Wrong password, try again** line on each retry. New rows
  **AP-01..AP-08**. Earlier: **2026-08-07d** — (**NAV-17/NAV-19** — dot-prefixed names (`._*` and friends)
  are excluded from the archive on the way out AND skipped on the way in. **NAV-17** — the
  Export Data panel now lists **10** rows,
  not 8: **Background Webs** (the `webs` folder, which had no Path Settings row either) and
  **Bibles XML** were both silently absent from a "whole data" backup. Bibles XML is
  app-managed (`bibles-data`, no directory setting) and archives **only its top-level
  `*.xml` files, case-insensitively** — the downloaded bible databases beside them are
  re-downloadable and would dominate every backup. Row count unchanged at **636**.
  Earlier: **2026-08-07c** — new **PM-115** — a lyric's `ol:Config` `- Attachments:`
  block now produces real slides at the tail of the Stage Previewer instead of blank ones:
  one item per link, built from the link verbatim with nothing fetched to measure it, and a
  `file:///…` attachment is used as the src rather than run through `pathToFileURL`.
  Row counts re-derived: PL was already stale at 99 (PL-100 exists), so the denominator goes
  634 → **636**.
  Earlier: **2026-08-07b** — **PL-59/PL-66/PL-67** corrected against the live app on

  purpose, `collisionPolicyBySettingName`), PL-59 now names the actual restricted menu (the
  run-sheet family — Reveal Original / Set Specific Screen / Disable / Add CC Elements — and
  NO Show on Screens), and PL-66 records the real traversal wording `Invalid archive file
path` plus the open defect where a manifest-less archive leaks a raw ENOENT.
  Earlier: **2026-08-07** — **PL-07/PL-08/PM-01/PM-11** reworked: lyrics no longer
  have their own selection or their own previewer tab — a `.owl` row is selected like any
  other document (solo) and the Documents previewer swaps its BODY to the lyric preview.
  Earlier: new **PL-100** + reworded **PL-71** — a run sheet can now put
  the SCREEN ITSELF up and down: **Screen: Show** / **Screen: Hide**, two screen actions that
  are about the window rather than about what is on it, so an unattended sheet can light the
  screen for the pre-service loop and darken it at the end. They are the one screen family
  that NAMES its screens — a checklist is asked BEFORE the line is written and stored in the
  ordinary `screenIds` pin, because "the selected screens" and a "which screen?" menu both
  mean the operator is at the machine. Neither hosts a CC element; both may BE one. The
  **Add Action** submenu is TWELVE rows now. Verified live 2026-08-06.
  Previous: **2026-08-06c** — reworded **PL-46** — in the floating preview only what wears
  `cursor: pointer` moves the run cursor; a click on the space around it (body padding, the gap
  between thumbnails, a card's margin ring, the divider) focuses the widget and nothing more.
  Previous: **2026-08-06b** — no row changes; the **presenting flow deep mode** scope set is
  now stated in this file: the argument `presentingFlow` is a tracked mode over
  **PL-10, PL-29, PL-32..76, PL-81..101** with its own 68-row denominator, not a focus area
  that trims the run. See the section below, SKILL.md §6f and test-plan §S20.
  Previous: **2026-08-06a** — new **PL-97/PL-98** — a fourth run action, **Keyboard
  Event** (⌨), a shortcut you set by PRESSING it (Ctrl/Shift only, at least one, unique per

  opens it), and **parked is now the only reason a line is passed by**; the preview panel takes
