  foreground clears behind one **Other Clear FG Items** row. Verified live 2026-08-06.
  Previous: (**PL-95**: a **Next: Timeout** may now be armed with a TIME
  OF DAY instead of a count of seconds — its question carries a `Seconds` / `At Time` chooser,
  the row reads `Next: Timeout (7:05 AM)`, and a time that has already gone by is refused with
  **The set time is already due** rather than counting round to tomorrow. Its menu entry is
  **Change Timing**; an interval, which cannot be armed with one, still says **Change Seconds**.
  Verified live 2026-08-05.
  Previous: **PL-46**: what the run lands on is now CENTRED when it would otherwise sit flush against the bottom edge, instead of always taking the least scrolling — an operator could not see what was coming next. Verified live 2026-08-05.
  Previous: **PL-96** fix: a **Jump to** landing on a `Next: Interval` /
  `Next: Timeout` now ARMS it — the guard against jump-chains was withholding every run
  action, which broke the looping set (interval walks the slides, jump at the end points
  back at the interval). Only another jump is withheld now. Verified live 2026-08-05.
  Previous: **PL-71/PL-74/PL-95/PL-96**: an action's BADGE now carries
  its icon's colour — the run family's badges (`⟳` `⏱` `⤴`) are glyphs of the same family as
  their icons, and the clears' `ALL`/`BG`/`SL`/`BB`/`FG` now read in their own tint too. A
  slide's `#3` is untinted as before. Verified live 2026-08-05. Previous: **PL-95**: the two
  clocks no longer answer to raw input at
  all — the run's SELECTION CHANGING is the one signal, cancelling a timeout and restarting
  an interval's count. An unintended click used to kill a countdown. Verified live
  2026-08-05. Previous: **PL-89** again: **Ctrl** now also lands a payload from
  ANOTHER panel as a new element at the hovered index (it used to be inert unless a row of
  this presenting flow was being dragged), and **PL-95/PL-96**: the three run actions no longer
  share one colour — timeout amber, interval teal, jump purple, and the preview's countdown
  pill follows the mode it is running. Verified live 2026-08-05. Previous:
  **PL-96**: a drop onto a `Next: Interval` / `Next: Timeout`
  now says **This element does not accept CC element** — it used to borrow the jump's "takes
  only one", which is a different refusal. Verified live 2026-08-05. Previous:
  **PL-89**: a drag onto an element row now takes **Ctrl**
  (⌘) to force the REORDER and **Alt** to force the ATTACH, so neither depends on hitting the
  7px band; the rule moved out of the component into the testable `toPresentingFlowRowDropKind`.
  Verified live 2026-08-05. Previous: new **PL-96** — a third run action, **Jump to** (⤴), which
  is the run sheet's GOTO and the one thing in it that does not move forward. It is armed
  not with a number but with **exactly one CC element**, and that CC is a POINTER rather
  than a follower: it may name ANY line the sheet lists, a **document** included (which can
  never be an ordinary CC), and the row's **Add CC Elements** entry disappears once it holds
  one. Triggering it selects that line and fires it — entering a document at its first
  slide — and it may not itself be attached to anything. A jump landing on another **Jump
  to** deliberately does not chain. Verified live 2026-08-05. Previous:
  **PL-95** + **PL-90**: a **Next: Timeout** may now be
  attached to a line as a **CC element** — "show this, and go on by yourself N seconds
  later" — while a **Next: Interval** may not, and is the one kind absent from a row's
  **Add CC Elements** list for a reason other than reaching no screen: nothing an operator
  does stops an interval, so one riding a slide would keep walking the sheet forward. A
  CC'd timeout fires only once its HOST has resolved its screens (a dismissed "which
  screen?" menu starts no countdown), it never asks for a screen of its own, and its CC row
  carries **Change Seconds** instead of **Set Specific Screen**. Verified live 2026-08-05.
  Previous: new **PL-95** + reworded **PL-71** — the **Add Action**
  menu gained a SECOND family of actions, one that drives the RUN instead of a screen:
  **Next: Interval** (⟳) and **Next: Timeout** (⏱). Both ask for a number of seconds when
  they are added (shown in the row's label, re-armed from the row's own **Change Seconds**),
  both only work while the floating preview is open on that presenting flow — anywhere else they
  toast **Open the presenting flow preview to use this action** — and neither reaches a screen at
  all: no **Apply on Screens**, no **Set Specific Screen**, no **Add CC Elements**, and they
  cannot be made a CC of anything. A timeout counts down once and forces the next selection,
  and ANY real click or key cancels it; an interval keeps forcing it and is cleared only by
  the widget's own ⊗ pill (or by the run ending). The submenu is now FIFTEEN entries.
  Verified live 2026-08-05. Previous: new **PL-94** + reworded **PL-34** — the floating
  preview's right-click menus are now **the same menus as the tree's**, built by one shared
  `presentingFlowItemMenuHelpers` rather than each drawing its own subset. An element's frame in the
  preview used to offer only **Show on Screens** + **Disable** + **Add CC Elements** (and
  nothing at all for an audio or an error element); it now carries the whole element menu —
  **Reveal Original**, **Set Specific Screen**, the four moves, **Duplicate**, **Choose
  Color**, **Remove from Presenting Flow** — and a slide THUMBNAIL gained the **Reveal Original** its
  tree row already had. Verified live 2026-08-05. Previous: new **PL-89..PL-93** + reworded **PL-55**/**PL-57**/**PL-88** —
  **CC elements**: anything in a presenting flow, child slides included, can now be attached to
  another line as a CC, and an element can hold many. A CC is drawn as a `↳` row under its
  host and rides onto the screens with it — one click puts a slide and its top marquee up
  together, and a CC on a document element rides with every slide of it. Attached by dropping
  onto a row or from **Add CC Elements**; clicking a CC never presents it, it reveals the
  element it is a copy of. **Dropping onto a row no longer appends an element to the
  presenting flow** — the name row and the empty state are the add targets now. Verified live
  2026-08-05. Previous: reworded **PL-86**/**PL-87** — a parked row now says WHICH
  park: the run sheet's own (an element, a slide's own flag, or a slide inheriting its parked
  document) strikes the label through and marks it with an amber `slash-circle`, while a
  slide the DOCUMENT hides keeps the grey `eye-slash`; the tooltips name the two apart and
  the floating preview's thumbnails gained the matching badge in their bottom-left corner.
  The two looked identical before, so a row an operator could release read exactly like one
  they could not. Verified live 2026-08-05. Previous: new **PL-88** + reworded **PL-34**/**PL-53**/**PL-72** —
  every presenting flow element's context menu gained **Move to Top** / **Move to Bottom** (the long
  jumps, gated exactly like **Move up** / **Move down**: an element already at that end is not
  offered the entry) and **Duplicate**, which drops a deep copy of the entry directly BELOW
  itself carrying its colour, its screen pins and its parked slides. Both moves reuse the
  existing `PresentingFlow.moveItemToIndex`; the copy is a new `PresentingFlow.duplicateItemAtIndex`, and
  because presenting flow entries carry no unique key nothing has to be re-keyed. Verified live
  2026-08-05. Previous: new **PL-86/PL-87** + reworded **PL-34** — every presenting flow
  element, and every slide of a document element, can now be **Disabled** (parked) from its
  context menu: the row dims and its click, its drag-to-screen and the next-key all pass it
  by, while its menu keeps the way back. The flag lives on the presenting flow ENTRY
  (`isDisabled` / `disabledSlideIds`), never on what it points at — a pdf/pptx slide has no
  writable flag of its own, and the same slide may be parked in one run sheet and live in
  another. Verified live 2026-08-05. Previous: new **PL-81..PL-85** — a presenting flow element, a document
  element, or ONE slide of a document can be PINNED to specific screens from
  **Set Specific Screen**, and every present the panel does then ignores the selected
  screens. "Which screens" is still answered in one place: `chooseScreenIds` gained a
  `presetScreenIds` argument that `isForceChoosing` outranks, so **Show on Screens** and a
  drag-onto-a-mini-screen keep overriding a pin. Verified live on three screens
  2026-08-05. Previous: new **PR-27..PR-29** + **CM-38/CM-39** — a **bible list**
  now exports/imports as a `.owbible.tar.gz` bundle. The single-item bundle moved to
  `src/helper/singleItemArchiveHelpers.ts` and is shared by the document and bible lists, so
  the manifest gained `itemKind` (each list refuses the other's bundle) and its root field
  was renamed `document` → `item` (the old name is still read). A **lyric** needs no rows of
  its own: it is a document row and rides PL-77..PL-80 — verified live 2026-08-05, its two
  attached slide backgrounds and its `.bg.json` round-tripped. Previous: new **PL-77..PL-80** + **CM-36/CM-37** — a single
  **document** now exports/imports as a `.owadoc.tar.gz` bundle exactly the way a presenting flow
  does, carrying its attached backgrounds, its canvas media and its color notes. The whole
  archive machinery moved to `src/helper/appArchiveHelpers.ts` and is shared with the
  presenting flow path, so the reuse-by-MD5, resolve-folders-first and never-clobber rules are
  literally the same code. Previous: **RD-03** corrected — the Bible **Reader** does NOT resolve
  a typed full reference; it book-filters exactly like the modal, because both surfaces render
  the same `InputHandlerComp` writing the same `BIBLE_LOOKUP_INPUT_ID`. Verified live
  2026-08-05: `John 3:16` showed only the John tile and picking it rewrote the input to
  `យ៉ូហាន `, dropping `3:16`. The old "reader page does" pass condition was doc drift and would
  have made a correct app FAIL. Previous: new **PL-76** and a rewritten **PL-67** — the presenting flow
  bundle now round-trips a document's OWN canvas media, and an import decides by CONTENTS
  rather than by name. A slide's **video box** stores a path (an image box inlines base64),
  so those files were never bundled and the imported slide showed an empty box; they now
  ride in the manifest as `video` entries and the copied document's `props.filePath` is
  re-pointed at the local copy. On import, a same-name local file is only REUSED when its
  MD5 matches — a different one is left alone and the archive's copy lands beside it as
  `1 (1).jpg`, since two machines can each hold a different `a.mp4`. Previous: new **PL-75** — the Presenting Flows list menu gained **Import
  From URL** under **Import**: it asks for a URL, streams the `.owapf.tar.gz` into a temp
  work dir and runs the SAME import as the file picker and the drop path, deleting the
  download afterwards. To make that reachable at all, `initHttpRequest` now picks
  `node:http` vs `node:https` from the URL's protocol and honours an explicit port — plain
  `http://` and any non-443 port were previously impossible for EVERY download in the app,
  so **CM-24**'s `Download From URL` inherits the fix. Previous: new **PL-74** — the **Add Action** menu now also offers a
  clear for ONE foreground widget at a time (marquee top/bottom, quick text, countdown,
  stopwatch, time, camera show, web show), each doing what that widget's own hide button
  in the Foreground panel does rather than wiping the layer; the panel's **Background
  Images Slide Show** deliberately has none, since it drives the BACKGROUND. **PL-71** now
  expects a thirteen-entry submenu. Previous: new **PL-71..PL-73** — a presenting flow can now hold a **screen
  action** as an element. The presenting flow's own context menu gained **Add Action**
  (បន្ថែមសកម្មភាព), whose submenu offers the five clears the mini screen's bar fires
  (**Clear All / Background / Slide / Bible / Foreground**); the element behaves like any
  other row (click, drag onto a mini screen, **Apply on Screens**, colour note, reorder,
  remove) except that it is RUN on a screen rather than shown there — so it carries no
  **Reveal Original**, never marks as on-screen, and its label is translated live instead
  of being captured on add. The floating preview draws it as the button that runs it, and
  the next-key stops on it and fires it. Previous: **the Presenting Flows panel is NO LONGER dev-only** — commit
  `203d35cc` dropped the `isDev` gate in `AppPresenterLeftComp` and gave the panel the slot
  the Lyric List used to hold (lyrics now live in the Documents list), so **every PL
  presenting flow row is in scope on a packaged build too**; the "dev only / else BLOCKED" note is
  gone from PL-10, PL-29 and PL-32..PL-48 and new **PL-49** pins the placement itself. Also
  new: **PL-50..PL-70**, a full second pass over the presenting flow surface that the first pass
  left implicit — placeholders and damaged entries (PL-50/51), colour notes (PL-52),
  per-document expansion memory and setting-name sanitising (PL-53/54), the drag rules
  including the cross-presenting-flow no-op (PL-55..57), the preview's fold memory / restricted
  slide menu / three ways to a screen / widget frame (PL-58..61), re-read-on-change and the
  unscoped-`select` perf guard (PL-62/63), lyric stage entries (PL-64), export/import edge
  cases — prerequisites, atomicity, traversal refusal, double import, bible-into-Default
  (PL-65..68) — and the two on-screen-marking subtleties (PL-69/70). Row counts corrected:
  the totals block had drifted 35 rows behind the table. Previous: **PL-48** now also pins that crossing INTO an element
  always opens it at its FIRST slide, and **PL-46** that the element label only marks the
  element — folding moved to the chevron. Previous: new **PL-48** — the preview's next-key now walks a
  **document element's own slides** first and only moves to the next element once the
  slide on screen is that document's last. Previous: new **PL-47** — the presenting flow floating preview gained
  **Collapse All / Expand All** icons pinned at its bottom-right, on the same baseline as
  the footer's `...` handle, each disabled when it has nothing left to fold or unfold. Previous: new **PL-46** — the presenting flow floating preview now
  answers **Space / ↓ / → / PageDown** by presenting the NEXT element after the one it
  remembers, gated on the widget holding focus. Previous: new **PL-45** — the Presenting Flows list now imports a
  `.owapf.tar.gz` **dropped onto it** from the OS file manager, the same import the
  list menu's **Import** entry runs (PL-40); any other dropped file still falls through
  to the plain copy-into-the-folder handling. Previous: **every file list now has exactly one button — a gray
  ⋮** (`bi-three-dots-vertical`, `title="More Options"`) opening the _same_ menu as
  right-clicking the empty card body. It sits in the card header where there is one
  (PL-11) and in the path row otherwise — background / foreground-web tabs pass no
  `header` (PL-15 / PM-99). The path row's **Reload** (`bi-arrow-clockwise`) and
  **Add items** (`bi-plus-lg`) icons are **gone**: Reload is now the first list-menu
  entry (PL-14 / PM-98) and Add Items was already one. New **SP-18** — the mini-screen
  previewer gained a matching floating ⋮ at the bottom-**right** of its card, mirroring
  the footer's auto-hide `...` handle at bottom-left. The Documents list's single
  `New File` entry split into **`New App Document`** (`.ows`) and **`New Lyric`**
  (`.owl`) as two top-level entries, replacing the old `New File` → `Documents`/`Lyrics`
  submenu — PL-24 and CM-23 updated. Single-kind lists (Bibles / Bible Notes /
  Presenting Flows) keep one `New File` entry. Previous: the **Presenting Flows** widget is now functional — PL-29 rewritten
  (add-by-drop for all five element kinds, references vs presets) and new **PL-32..PL-40**:
  document-row expansion, click-to-show, element context menu, drag-to-screen, in-list
  reorder, **Reveal Original** in the element context menu (PL-37 — it replaced the
  3-second hover-to-locate, and PL-34 lists it), the floating full preview, Export / Import of a
  `.owapf.tar.gz` bundle, **PL-41** the on-screen `*` indicator at every level, **PL-42** preview zoom (slider / Ctrl+wheel / pinch), **PL-43** audio entries (drag in, click to reveal in the Audios split — never an inline player), and **PL-44** per-type document icons. (It was still dev-only at that point; it no longer
  is — see the head of this note.) Previous: new **PM-114** — the background tabs' thumbnail/list view
  toggle in `BackgroundFooterComp`, per-tab persisted; PM-101 refined to note the size slider
  is hidden in list mode. Previous: **SC-08** — lyric previewer↔screen render parity, to be
  measured on a **cold start**: lyric slide HTML comes from the `open-lyric` dep and its
  render options are populated only by a presenter component, so the projector can show
  open-lyric's raw 16px default while the operator's preview looks correct, and the mismatch
  "heals" after a re-present. See knowledge-base §13. Previous: **MD-01..03** group — media download from URL
  (video + audio) is now part of the mandatory core, with a canonical test link; the
  media helpers yt-dlp/ffmpeg/qjs are installed on demand into `extra-bin/` (MD-05), so a
  download failure can mean a missing binary, not just a network problem. Previous: **RD-23**/**CM-58** rewritten: the MS Word export no
  longer goes through the .NET helper or a save dialog — it writes the `.docx` straight
  into Downloads, reveals it, and asks about exporting fonts only when the looked-up
  bibles' language ships any. **LT-01..02** are in the mandatory core — a locale switch is
  required in every run; see §LT for why an English-only run is blind. **GL-23**: toasts
  stack in `.app-toast-stack` instead of replacing each other, so GL-10/GL-15 use per-toast
  timers)
